Indeed, instead of broadcasting the compressed messages
S^{k}
in
Algorithms

1
,
2
and
3
, the server can compute
G^{\sharp}
, transmit this naturally compressed object, and let the workers perform the model update locally.
In doing so, we preserve communication efficiency while avoiding the introduction of additional primal compressors.

Appendix E
Convergence Analysis

E.1
Descent Lemmas

We provide two descent lemmas corresponding to the two smoothness regimes. The first applies to the layer-wise smooth setting.

Lemma 1

(Descent Lemma I)
.

Let
6
hold and consider the update rule
X_{i}^{k+1}=X_{i}^{k}-\gamma_{i}^{k}\left(G_{i}^{k}\right)^{\sharp}
,
i=1,\ldots,p
, where
X^{k+1}=[X_{1}^{k+1},\ldots,X_{p}^{k+1}],X^{k}=[X_{1}^{k},\ldots,X_{p}^{k}],G^{k}=[G_{1}^{k},\ldots,G_{p}^{k}]\in\mathcal{S}
and
\gamma_{i}^{k}>0
. Then

\displaystyle f(X^{k+1})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle f(X^{k})+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{3\gamma_{i}^{k}}{2}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-G_{i}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\gamma_{i}^{k}}{4}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{1}{4\gamma_{i}^{k}}-\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{2}\right)(\gamma_{i}^{k})^{2}\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}.

Proof.

First, for any
s>0
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-G_{i}^{k}+G_{i}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:young}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1+s\right)\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-G_{i}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}+\left(1+\frac{1}{s}\right)\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star},

meaning that

\displaystyle-\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1+s}{1+\frac{1}{s}}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-G_{i}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}-\frac{1}{1+\frac{1}{s}}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}

(10)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle s\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-G_{i}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}-\frac{s}{s+1}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}.

Then, using layer-wise smoothness of
f
and
Lemma

14
with
L_{i}^{1}=0
, we get

\displaystyle f(X^{k+1})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle f(X^{k})+\left\langle\nabla f(X^{k}),X^{k+1}-X^{k}\right\rangle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{L^{0}_{i}}{2}\left\|X_{i}^{k}-X_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f(X^{k})+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left\langle\nabla_{i}f(X^{k}),X_{i}^{k+1}-X_{i}^{k}\right\rangle_{(i)}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{L^{0}_{i}}{2}\left\|X_{i}^{k}-X_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f(X^{k})-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\gamma_{i}^{k}\left\langle\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-G_{i}^{k},\left(G_{i}^{k}\right)^{\sharp}\right\rangle_{(i)}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\gamma_{i}^{k}\left\langle G_{i}^{k},\left(G_{i}^{k}\right)^{\sharp}\right\rangle_{(i)}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{L^{0}_{i}}{2}(\gamma_{i}^{k})^{2}\left\|\left(G_{i}^{k}\right)^{\sharp}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:inpsharp},\eqref{eq:normsharp}}{=}

\displaystyle f(X^{k})-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\gamma_{i}^{k}\left\langle\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-G_{i}^{k},\left(G_{i}^{k}\right)^{\sharp}\right\rangle_{(i)}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\gamma_{i}^{k}}{2}\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\gamma_{i}^{k}}{2}\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{L^{0}_{i}}{2}(\gamma_{i}^{k})^{2}\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:oaisbgnv}}{\leq}

\displaystyle f(X^{k})-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\gamma_{i}^{k}\left\langle\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-G_{i}^{k},\left(G_{i}^{k}\right)^{\sharp}\right\rangle-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\gamma_{i}^{k}}{2}\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\gamma_{i}^{k}}{2}s\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-G_{i}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\gamma_{i}^{k}}{2}\frac{s}{s+1}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{2}(\gamma_{i}^{k})^{2}\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}.

Therefore, applying Fenchel’s inequality, we get

\displaystyle\hskip-22.76219ptf(X^{k+1})

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:fenchel}}{\leq}

\displaystyle f(X^{k})+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{\gamma_{i}^{k}}{2r}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-G_{i}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}+\frac{\gamma_{i}^{k}r}{2}\left\|\left(G_{i}^{k}\right)^{\sharp}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}-\frac{\gamma_{i}^{k}}{2}\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{\gamma_{i}^{k}}{2}s\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-G_{i}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}-\frac{\gamma_{i}^{k}}{2}\frac{s}{s+1}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}+\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{2}(\gamma_{i}^{k})^{2}\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f(X^{k})+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{\gamma_{i}^{k}}{2r}+\frac{\gamma_{i}^{k}s}{2}\right)\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-G_{i}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\gamma_{i}^{k}}{2}\frac{s}{s+1}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{1-r}{2\gamma_{i}^{k}}-\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{2}\right)(\gamma_{i}^{k})^{2}\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}

for any
r>0
. Choosing
s=1
and
r=\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}
finishes the proof.
∎

The next lemma is specific to the layer-wise smooth case.

Lemma 2

(Descent Lemma II)
.

Let
8
hold and consider the update rule
X_{i}^{k+1}={\rm LMO}_{\mathcal{B}(X_{i}^{k},t_{i}^{k})}\left(G_{i}^{k}\right)
,
i=1,\ldots,p
, where
X^{k+1}=[X_{1}^{k+1},\ldots,X_{p}^{k+1}],X^{k}=[X_{1}^{k},\ldots,X_{p}^{k}],G^{k}=[G_{1}^{k},\ldots,G_{p}^{k}]\in\mathcal{S}
and
t_{i}^{k}>0
. Then

\displaystyle f(X^{k+1})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle f(X^{k})+\sum_{i=1}^{p}2t_{i}^{k}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}^{k}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{L^{0}_{i}+L^{1}_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}}{2}(t_{i}^{k})^{2}.

Proof.

8
and
Lemma

14
give

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91692ptf(X^{k+1})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle f(X^{k})+\left\langle\nabla f(X^{k}),X^{k+1}-X^{k}\right\rangle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{L^{0}_{i}+L^{1}_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}}{2}\left\|X_{i}^{k}-X_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f(X^{k})+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left\langle\nabla_{i}f(X^{k}),X_{i}^{k+1}-X_{i}^{k}\right\rangle_{(i)}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{L^{0}_{i}+L^{1}_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}}{2}\left\|X_{i}^{k}-X_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f(X^{k})+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\left\langle\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-G_{i}^{k},X_{i}^{k+1}-X_{i}^{k}\right\rangle_{(i)}+\left\langle G_{i}^{k},X_{i}^{k+1}-X_{i}^{k}\right\rangle_{(i)}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{L^{0}_{i}+L^{1}_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}}{2}(t_{i}^{k})^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:inplmo}}{=}

\displaystyle f(X^{k})+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\left\langle\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-G_{i}^{k},X_{i}^{k+1}-X_{i}^{k}\right\rangle_{(i)}-t_{i}^{k}\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{L^{0}_{i}+L^{1}_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}}{2}(t_{i}^{k})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle f(X^{k})+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(t_{i}^{k}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}-t_{i}^{k}\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}+\frac{L^{0}_{i}+L^{1}_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}}{2}(t_{i}^{k})^{2}\right),

where the last line follows from the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and the fact that
\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-X_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)}=t_{i}^{k}
.
Therefore, using triangle inequality, we get

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91692ptf(X^{k+1})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle f(X^{k})+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(t_{i}^{k}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}+t_{i}^{k}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}-t_{i}^{k}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{L^{0}_{i}+L^{1}_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}}{2}(t_{i}^{k})^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f(X^{k})+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(2t_{i}^{k}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}-t_{i}^{k}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{L^{0}_{i}+L^{1}_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}}{2}(t_{i}^{k})^{2}.

∎

E.2
Auxiliary Lemmas

Lemma 3
.

The iterates of Algorithm
2
and
3
run with
\mathcal{C}_{i}^{k}\in\mathbb{B}(\alpha_{P})
satisfy

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right]\leq\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{P}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k}-W_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right]+\frac{2}{\alpha_{P}}(\gamma_{i}^{k})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right].

Proof.

Let
{\mathbb{E}}_{\mathcal{C}}\left[\cdot\right]
denote the expectation over the randomness introduced by the compressors. Then

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt{\mathbb{E}}_{\mathcal{C}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\mathcal{C}}\left[\left\|W_{i}^{k}+\mathcal{C}_{i}^{k}(X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k})-X_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{def:contractive_compressor}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1-\alpha_{P})\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:young}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1-\alpha_{P})\left(1+\frac{\alpha_{P}}{2}\right)\left\|X_{i}^{k}-W_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}+(1-\alpha_{P})\left(1+\frac{2}{\alpha_{P}}\right)\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-X_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:ineq1},\eqref{eq:ineq2}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{P}}{2}\right)\left\|X_{i}^{k}-W_{i}^{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{2}{\alpha_{P}}\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-X_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}.

It remains to take full expectation and use the fact that

\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-X_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)}=\gamma_{i}^{k}\left\|\left(G_{i}^{k}\right)^{\sharp}\right\|_{(i)}\overset{\eqref{eq:normsharp}}{=}\gamma_{i}^{k}\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}.

∎

E.2.1
Smooth Case

Lemma 4
.

Let Assumptions
7
and
10
hold. Then, the iterates of
Algorithm

3
run with
\mathcal{C}_{i,j}^{k}\in\mathbb{B}_{2}(\alpha_{P})
satisfy

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{k+1}-G_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{D}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{k}-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]+\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{k}-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}(L_{i,j}^{0})^{2}(\gamma_{i}^{k})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{\star}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}(L_{i,j}^{0})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right]+(1-\alpha_{D})\beta_{i}^{2}\sigma_{i}^{2}.

Proof.

Using the definition of contractive compressors and the algorithm’s momentum update rule, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\mathcal{C}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{k+1}-G_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\mathcal{C}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{k+1}-G_{i,j}^{k}-\mathcal{C}_{i,j}^{k}(M_{i,j}^{k+1}-G_{i,j}^{k})\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{def:contractive_compressor}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1-\alpha_{D})\left\|M_{i,j}^{k+1}-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2},

where
{\mathbb{E}}_{\mathcal{C}}\left[\cdot\right]
denotes the expectation over the randomness introduced by the compressors. Then, letting
{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi}\left[\cdot\right]
be the expectation over the stochasticity of the gradients, we have

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91684pt{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{k+1}-G_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\mathbb{E}}_{\mathcal{C}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{k+1}-G_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\alpha_{D}){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{k+1}-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\alpha_{D}){\mathbb{E}}\left[{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi}\left[\left\|(1-\beta_{i})M_{i,j}^{k}+\beta_{i}\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1};\xi_{j}^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{lemma:vardecomp}}{=}

\displaystyle(1-\alpha_{D}){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|(1-\beta_{i})M_{i,j}^{k}+\beta_{i}\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle+(1-\alpha_{D})\beta_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1};\xi_{j}^{k+1})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1})\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:young}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1-\alpha_{D})\left(1+\frac{\alpha_{D}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{k}-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle+(1-\alpha_{D})\left(1+\frac{2}{\alpha_{D}}\right)\beta_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{k}-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1})\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]+(1-\alpha_{D})\beta_{i}^{2}\sigma_{i}^{2},

where in the last line we used
10
. Then,
7
gives

\displaystyle\hskip-34.14322pt{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{k+1}-G_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:ineq1},\eqref{eq:ineq2}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{D}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{k}-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]+\frac{2}{\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{k}-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1})\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]+(1-\alpha_{D})\beta_{i}^{2}\sigma_{i}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:young}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{D}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{k}-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]+\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{k}-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1})\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]+(1-\alpha_{D})\beta_{i}^{2}\sigma_{i}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{D}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{k}-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]+\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{k}-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}(L_{i,j}^{0})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k}-X_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}(L_{i,j}^{0})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right]+(1-\alpha_{D})\beta_{i}^{2}\sigma_{i}^{2}.

Noting that
\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-X_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)}=\gamma_{i}^{k}\left\|\left(G_{i}^{k}\right)^{\sharp}\right\|_{(i)}\overset{\eqref{eq:normsharp}}{=}\gamma_{i}^{k}\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}
finishes the proof.
∎

Lemma 5
.

Let Assumptions
6
,
7
and
10
hold. Then, the iterates of
Algorithm

3
satisfy

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-M_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\beta_{i}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-M_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}(L_{i,j}^{0})^{2}(\gamma_{i}^{k})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{\beta_{i}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right)(L_{i,j}^{0})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right]+\beta_{i}^{2}\sigma_{i}^{2}

and

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k+1})-M_{i}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\beta_{i}}{2}\right)\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-M_{i}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}(L_{i}^{0})^{2}(\gamma_{i}^{k})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{\beta_{i}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right)(L_{i}^{0})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right]+\frac{\beta_{i}^{2}\sigma_{i}^{2}}{n},

where
M_{i}^{k}:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}M_{i,j}^{k}
.

Proof.

Using the momentum update rule and letting
{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi}\left[\cdot\right]
be the expectation over the stochasticity of the gradients, we get

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-M_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-(1-\beta_{i})M_{i,j}^{k}-\beta_{i}\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1};\xi_{j}^{k+1})\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{lemma:vardecomp}}{=}

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-(1-\beta_{i})M_{i,j}^{k}-\beta_{i}\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1})\right\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle+\beta_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1};\xi_{j}^{k+1})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1})\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:young}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1-\beta_{i})^{2}\left(1+\frac{\beta_{i}}{2}\right)\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-M_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle+\beta_{i}^{2}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right)\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1})\right\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle+\beta_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1};\xi_{j}^{k+1})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1})\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:ineq1}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1-\beta_{i})\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-M_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle+\beta_{i}^{2}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right)\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1})\right\|^{2}_{2}+\beta_{i}^{2}\sigma_{i}^{2},

where in the last line we used
5
. Then,
7
gives

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-M_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-M_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:young}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1-\beta_{i})\left(1+\frac{\beta_{i}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-M_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle+(1-\beta_{i})\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\beta_{i}^{2}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1})\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]+\beta_{i}^{2}\sigma_{i}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:ineq1},\eqref{eq:ineq2}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\beta_{i}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-M_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}(L_{i,j}^{0})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-X_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{\beta_{i}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right)(L_{i,j}^{0})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right]+\beta_{i}^{2}\sigma_{i}^{2}.

To prove the second part of the statement, define
\nabla_{i}f(X;\xi^{k}):=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X;\xi_{j}^{k})
. Then
M_{i}^{k+1}=(1-\beta_{i})M_{i}^{k}+\beta_{i}\nabla_{i}f(W^{k+1};\xi^{k+1})
, so following similar steps as above, we get

\displaystyle\hskip-22.76228pt{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k+1})-M_{i}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k+1})-(1-\beta_{i})M_{i}^{k}-\beta_{i}\nabla_{i}f(W^{k+1};\xi^{k+1})\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{lemma:vardecomp}}{=}

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k+1})-(1-\beta_{i})M_{i}^{k}-\beta_{i}\nabla_{i}f(W^{k+1})\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\beta_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(W^{k+1};\xi^{k+1})-\nabla_{i}f(W^{k+1})\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:young}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1-\beta_{i})^{2}\left(1+\frac{\beta_{i}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k+1})-M_{i}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\beta_{i}^{2}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k+1})-\nabla_{i}f(W^{k+1})\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\beta_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(W^{k+1};\xi^{k+1})-\nabla_{i}f(W^{k+1})\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:ineq1}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1-\beta_{i}){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k+1})-M_{i}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\beta_{i}^{2}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k+1})-\nabla_{i}f(W^{k+1})\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]+\frac{\beta_{i}^{2}\sigma_{i}^{2}}{n}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:young}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1-\beta_{i})\left(1+\frac{\beta_{i}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-M_{i}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle+(1-\beta_{i})\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k+1})-\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\beta_{i}^{2}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k+1})-\nabla_{i}f(W^{k+1})\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]+\frac{\beta_{i}^{2}\sigma_{i}^{2}}{n}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:ineq1},\eqref{eq:ineq2}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\beta_{i}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-M_{i}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}(L_{i}^{0})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-X_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{\beta_{i}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right)(L_{i}^{0})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right]+\frac{\beta_{i}^{2}\sigma_{i}^{2}}{n}.

It remains to use the fact that
\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-X_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)}=\gamma_{i}^{k}\left\|\left(G_{i}^{k}\right)^{\sharp}\right\|_{(i)}\overset{\eqref{eq:normsharp}}{=}\gamma_{i}^{k}\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}
.
∎

E.2.2
Generalized Smooth Case

Lemma 6
.

Let
9
hold. Then, the iterates of
Algorithm

2
run with
\mathcal{C}_{i}^{k}\equiv\mathcal{I}
(the identity compressor) and
\mathcal{C}_{i,j}^{k}\in\mathbb{B}_{\star}(\alpha_{D})
satisfy

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)\star}\right|X^{k+1},G^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}+\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\left(L^{0}_{i,j}+L^{1}_{i,j}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)t_{i}^{k}.

Proof.

The algorithm’s update rule and Jensen’s inequality give

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)\star}\right|X^{k+1},G^{k}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\sqrt{\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k}-\mathcal{C}_{i,j}^{k}(\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}}\right|X^{k+1},G^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k}-\mathcal{C}_{i,j}^{k}(\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right|X^{k+1},G^{k}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}+\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\left(L^{0}_{i,j}+L^{1}_{i,j}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-X_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)}.

where
\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-X_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)}=t_{i}^{k}
.
∎

Lemma 7
.

Let Assumptions
9
and
10
hold. Then, the iterates of
Algorithm

3
run with
\mathcal{C}_{i}^{k}\equiv\mathcal{I}
(the identity compressor) and
\mathcal{C}_{i,j}^{k}\in\mathbb{B}_{2}(\alpha_{D})
satisfy

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|M_{i,j}^{k+1}-G_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|_{2}\right|X^{k+1},M_{i,j}^{k},G_{i,j}^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\left\|M_{i,j}^{k}-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|_{2}+\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}\left\|M_{i,j}^{k}-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\left(L^{0}_{i,j}+L^{1}_{i,j}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)t_{i}^{k}+\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}\sigma_{i}.

Proof.

Using the definition of contractive compressors and triangle inequality, we get

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|M_{i,j}^{k+1}-G_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|_{2}\right|M_{i,j}^{k+1},G_{i,j}^{k}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\sqrt{\left\|M_{i,j}^{k+1}-G_{i,j}^{k}-\mathcal{C}_{i,j}^{k}(M_{i,j}^{k+1}-G_{i,j}^{k})\right\|^{2}_{2}}\right|M_{i,j}^{k+1},G_{i,j}^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|M_{i,j}^{k+1}-G_{i,j}^{k}-\mathcal{C}_{i,j}^{k}(M_{i,j}^{k+1}-G_{i,j}^{k})\right\|^{2}_{2}\right|M_{i,j}^{k+1},G_{i,j}^{k}\right]}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{def:contractive_compressor}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\left\|M_{i,j}^{k+1}-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\left\|(1-\beta_{i})M_{i,j}^{k}+\beta_{i}\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1};\xi_{j}^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|_{2}.

Hence,

\displaystyle\hskip-22.76228pt{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|M_{i,j}^{k+1}-G_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|_{2}\right|X^{k+1},M_{i,j}^{k},G_{i,j}^{k}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|M_{i,j}^{k+1}-G_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|_{2}\right|M_{i,j}^{k+1},G_{i,j}^{k}\right]\right|X^{k+1},M_{i,j}^{k},G_{i,j}^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|(1-\beta_{i})M_{i,j}^{k}+\beta_{i}\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1};\xi_{j}^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|_{2}\right|X^{k+1},M_{i,j}^{k},G_{i,j}^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|(1-\beta_{i})M_{i,j}^{k}+\beta_{i}\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|_{2}\right|X^{k+1},M_{i,j}^{k},G_{i,j}^{k}\right]

\displaystyle+\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1};\xi_{j}^{k+1})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})\right\|_{2}\right|X^{k+1},M_{i,j}^{k},G_{i,j}^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{as:bounded_layer_var}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\left\|M_{i,j}^{k}-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|_{2}+\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}\left\|M_{i,j}^{k}-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})\right\|_{2}+\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}\sigma_{i}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\left\|M_{i,j}^{k}-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|_{2}+\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}\left\|M_{i,j}^{k}-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})\right\|_{2}+\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}\sigma_{i}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{as:workers_layer_gen_smoothness}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\left\|M_{i,j}^{k}-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|_{2}+\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}\left\|M_{i,j}^{k}-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\left(L^{0}_{i,j}+L^{1}_{i,j}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)\left\|X_{i}^{k}-X_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}+\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}\sigma_{i}.

Using the fact that
\left\|X_{i}^{k}-X_{i}^{k+1}\right\|=t_{i}^{k}
finishes the proof.
∎

Lemma 8
.

Let Assumptions
8
,
9
and
10
hold. Then, the iterates of
Algorithm

3
run with
\mathcal{C}_{i}^{k}\equiv\mathcal{I}
(the identity compressor) and
t_{i}^{k}\equiv t_{i}
satisfy

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i}^{k+1}-\nabla_{i}f(X^{k+1})\right\|_{2}\right]\leq

\displaystyle\,(1-\beta_{i})^{k+1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i}^{0}-\nabla_{i}f(X^{0})\right\|_{2}\right]+\frac{t_{i}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\beta_{i}\underline{\rho}_{i}}

\displaystyle+\frac{t_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}L^{1}_{i,j}\sum_{l=0}^{k}(1-\beta_{i})^{k+1-l}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{l})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]+\sigma_{i}\sqrt{\frac{\beta_{i}}{n}}

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{k+1}-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})\right\|_{2}\right]\leq

\displaystyle\,(1-\beta_{i})\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{k}-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{2}\right]+t_{i}\frac{(1-\beta_{i})\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}

\displaystyle+t_{i}\frac{1-\beta_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}L^{1}_{i,j}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]+\beta_{i}\sigma_{i},

where
M_{i}^{k}:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}M_{i,j}^{k}
.

Proof.

The proof uses techniques similar to those in
Cutkosky and Mehta 2020
.
First, using the momentum update rule, we can write

\displaystyle M_{i,j}^{k+1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\beta_{i})M_{i,j}^{k}+\beta_{i}\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1};\xi_{j}^{k+1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\beta_{i})\left(M_{i,j}^{k}-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right)+(1-\beta_{i})\left(\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})\right)

\displaystyle+\beta_{i}\left(\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1};\xi_{j}^{k+1})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})\right)+\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1}),

and hence

\displaystyle U_{1,i,j}^{k+1}=(1-\beta_{i})U_{1,i,j}^{k}+(1-\beta_{i})U_{2,i,j}^{k}+\beta_{i}U_{3,i,j}^{k+1},

where we define
U_{1,i,j}^{k}:=M_{i,j}^{k}-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})
,
U_{2,i,j}^{k}:=\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})
and
U_{3,i,j}^{k}:=\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k};\xi_{j}^{k})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})
. Unrolling the recursion gives

\displaystyle U_{1,i,j}^{k+1}=(1-\beta_{i})^{k+1}U_{1,i,j}^{0}+\sum_{l=0}^{k}(1-\beta_{i})^{k+1-l}U_{2,i,j}^{l}+\beta_{i}\sum_{l=0}^{k}(1-\beta_{i})^{k-l}U_{3,i,j}^{l+1}.

Hence, using the triangle inequality,

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91684pt{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}U_{1,i,j}^{k+1}\right\|_{2}\right]

(11)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\beta_{i})^{k+1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}U_{1,i,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right]+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\sum_{l=0}^{k}(1-\beta_{i})^{k+1-l}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}U_{2,i,j}^{l}\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\beta_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\sum_{l=0}^{k}(1-\beta_{i})^{k-l}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}U_{3,i,j}^{l+1}\right\|_{2}\right].

Let us now bound the last two terms of the inequality above. First, triangle inequality and
9
give

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91684pt{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\sum_{l=0}^{k}(1-\beta_{i})^{k+1-l}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}U_{2,i,j}^{l}\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{l=0}^{k}(1-\beta_{i})^{k+1-l}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|U_{2,i,j}^{l}\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{l=0}^{k}(1-\beta_{i})^{k+1-l}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{l})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{l+1})\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{as:workers_layer_gen_smoothness}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{l=0}^{k}(1-\beta_{i})^{k+1-l}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(L^{0}_{i,j}+L^{1}_{i,j}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{l})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)\left\|X_{i}^{l}-X_{i}^{l+1}\right\|_{(i)}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{t_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{l=0}^{k}(1-\beta_{i})^{k+1-l}L^{0}_{i,j}+\frac{t_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}L^{1}_{i,j}\sum_{l=0}^{k}(1-\beta_{i})^{k+1-l}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{l})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{t_{i}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\beta_{i}\underline{\rho}_{i}}+\frac{t_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}L^{1}_{i,j}\sum_{l=0}^{k}(1-\beta_{i})^{k+1-l}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{l})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right],

and using Jensen’s inequality, the last term can be bounded as

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91684pt{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\sum_{l=0}^{k}(1-\beta_{i})^{k-l}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}U_{3,i,j}^{l+1}\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\sum_{l=0}^{k}(1-\beta_{i})^{k-l}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}U_{3,i,j}^{l+1}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]}\overset{\eqref{as:bounded_layer_var}}{=}\sqrt{\sum_{l=0}^{k}(1-\beta_{i})^{2(k-l)}\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|U_{3,i,j}^{l+1}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{as:bounded_layer_var}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\sqrt{\sum_{l=0}^{k}(1-\beta_{i})^{2(k-l)}\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sigma_{i}^{2}}=\frac{\sigma_{i}}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{\sum_{l=0}^{k}(1-\beta_{i})^{2l}}\leq\frac{\sigma_{i}}{\sqrt{n\beta_{i}(2-\beta_{i})}}\leq\frac{\sigma_{i}}{\sqrt{n\beta_{i}}}.

Substituting this in (
11
) yields

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}U_{1,i,j}^{k+1}\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\beta_{i})^{k+1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}U_{1,i,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right]+\frac{t_{i}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\beta_{i}\underline{\rho}_{i}}

\displaystyle+\frac{t_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}L^{1}_{i,j}\sum_{l=0}^{k}(1-\beta_{i})^{k+1-l}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{l})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]+\beta_{i}\frac{\sigma_{i}}{\sqrt{n\beta_{i}}}.

To prove the second inequality, recall that
U_{1,i,j}^{k+1}=(1-\beta_{i})U_{1,i,j}^{k}+(1-\beta_{i})U_{2,i,j}^{k}+\beta_{i}U_{3,i,j}^{k+1}
. Hence, taking norms, averaging, and using the triangle inequality,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|U_{1,i,j}^{k+1}\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\beta_{i})\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|U_{1,i,j}^{k}\right\|_{2}\right]+(1-\beta_{i})\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|U_{2,i,j}^{k}\right\|_{2}\right]

(12)

\displaystyle+\beta_{i}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|U_{3,i,j}^{k+1}\right\|_{2}\right],

where the last two terms can be bounded as

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|U_{2,i,j}^{k}\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{as:workers_layer_gen_smoothness}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(L^{0}_{i,j}+L^{1}_{i,j}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)\left\|X_{i}^{k}-X_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle t_{i}\frac{\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}+\frac{t_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}L^{1}_{i,j}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|U_{3,i,j}^{k+1}\right\|_{2}\right]=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1};\xi_{j}^{k})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})\right\|_{2}\right]\overset{\eqref{as:bounded_layer_var}}{\leq}\sigma_{i}.

It remains to substitute this in (
12
) to obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|U_{1,i,j}^{k+1}\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\beta_{i})\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|U_{1,i,j}^{k}\right\|_{2}\right]+t_{i}\frac{(1-\beta_{i})\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}

\displaystyle+t_{i}\frac{1-\beta_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}L^{1}_{i,j}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]+\beta_{i}\sigma_{i}.

∎

Lemma 9
.

Let Assumptions
1
and
8
hold. Then

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}}{2\left(L_{i}^{0}+L_{i}^{1}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)}\leq f(X)-f^{\star}

for any
X=[X_{1},\ldots,X_{p}]\in\mathcal{S}
.

Proof.

Let
Y=[Y_{1},\ldots,Y_{p}]\in\mathcal{S}
, where
Y_{i}=X_{i}-\frac{\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}}{L_{i}^{0}+L_{i}^{1}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}}H_{i}
for some
H_{i}\in\partial\left\|\cdot\right\|_{(i)\star}(\nabla_{i}f(X))
. Then,
Lemma

14
and the definition of subdifferential give

\displaystyle f(Y)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle f(X)+\left\langle\nabla f(X),Y-X\right\rangle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{L^{0}_{i}+L^{1}_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}}{2}\left\|X_{i}-Y_{i}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f(X)+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left\langle\nabla_{i}f(X),Y_{i}-X_{i}\right\rangle_{(i)}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{L^{0}_{i}+L^{1}_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}}{2}\left\|X_{i}-Y_{i}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f(X)-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}}{L_{i}^{0}+L_{i}^{1}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}}\left\langle\nabla_{i}f(X),H_{i}\right\rangle_{(i)}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{L^{0}_{i}+L^{1}_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}}{2}\frac{\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}}{\left(L_{i}^{0}+L_{i}^{1}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)^{2}}\left\|H_{i}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:subdiff}}{=}

\displaystyle f(X)+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(-\frac{\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}}{L_{i}^{0}+L_{i}^{1}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}}+\frac{\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}}{2\left(L_{i}^{0}+L_{i}^{1}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f(X)-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}}{2\left(L_{i}^{0}+L_{i}^{1}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)},

and hence

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}}{2\left(L_{i}^{0}+L_{i}^{1}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)}\leq f(X)-f(Y)\leq f(X)-f^{\star}

as needed.
∎

Lemma 10
.

Let Assumptions
1
and
8
hold. Then, for any
x_{i}>0
,
i\in[p]
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}\leq 4\max_{i\in[p]}(x_{i}L_{i}^{1})\left(f(X)-f^{\star}\right)+\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}^{2}L_{i}^{0}}{\max_{i\in[p]}(x_{i}L_{i}^{1})}

for all
X\in\mathcal{S}
.

Proof.

We follow an approach similar to that in
Khirirat et al. 2024
.
Applying
Lemma

9
and
Lemma

12
with
y_{i}=\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}
,
z_{i}=L_{i}^{0}+L_{i}^{1}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}
and any positive
x_{i}
, we have

\displaystyle 2\left(f(X)-f^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}}{L_{i}^{0}+L_{i}^{1}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)^{2}}{\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}^{2}L_{i}^{0}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}^{2}L_{i}^{1}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)^{2}}{\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}^{2}L_{i}^{0}+\max_{i\in[p]}(x_{i}L_{i}^{1})\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)^{2}}{2\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}^{2}L_{i}^{0}}&\textnormal{if }\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}^{2}L_{i}^{0}}{\max_{i\in[p]}(x_{i}L_{i}^{1})}\geq\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star},\\
\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}}{2\max_{i\in[p]}(x_{i}L_{i}^{1})}&\textnormal{otherwise}.\end{cases}

Therefore,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max\left\{4\max_{i\in[p]}(x_{i}L_{i}^{1})\left(f(X)-f^{\star}\right),\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}^{2}L_{i}^{0}}{\max_{i\in[p]}(x_{i}L_{i}^{1})}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\max_{i\in[p]}(x_{i}L_{i}^{1})\left(f(X)-f^{\star}\right)+\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}^{2}L_{i}^{0}}{\max_{i\in[p]}(x_{i}L_{i}^{1})}.

∎

Lemma 11
.

Let Assumptions
1
,
2
and
9
hold. Then, for any
x_{i}>0
,
i\in[p]
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\max_{i\in[p]}(x_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})\left(f_{j}(X)-f^{\star}\right)+4\max_{i\in[p]}(x_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})\left(f^{\star}-f_{j}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}^{2}L_{i,j}^{0}}{\max_{i\in[p]}(x_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})}

for all
X\in\mathcal{S}
.

Proof.

The proof is similar to that of
Lemma

10
. Applying
Lemma

9
and
Lemma

12
with
y_{i}=\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}
,
z_{i}=L_{i,j}^{0}+L_{i,j}^{1}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}
and any positive
x_{i}
, we have

\displaystyle 2\left(f_{j}(X)-f_{j}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X)\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}}{L_{i,j}^{0}+L_{i,j}^{1}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)^{2}}{\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}^{2}L_{i,j}^{0}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}^{2}L_{i,j}^{1}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)^{2}}{\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}^{2}L_{i,j}^{0}+\max_{i\in[p]}(x_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)^{2}}{2\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}^{2}L_{i,j}^{0}}&\textnormal{if }\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}^{2}L_{i,j}^{0}}{\max_{i\in[p]}(x_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})}\geq\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X)\right\|_{(i)\star},\\
\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}}{2\max_{i\in[p]}(x_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})}&\textnormal{otherwise}.\end{cases}

Therefore,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max\left\{4\max_{i\in[p]}(x_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})\left(f_{j}(X)-f_{j}^{\star}\right),\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}^{2}L_{i,j}^{0}}{\max_{i\in[p]}(x_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\max_{i\in[p]}(x_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})\left(f_{j}(X)-f_{j}^{\star}\right)+\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}^{2}L_{i,j}^{0}}{\max_{i\in[p]}(x_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4\max_{i\in[p]}(x_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})\left(f_{j}(X)-f^{\star}\right)+4\max_{i\in[p]}(x_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})\left(f^{\star}-f_{j}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}^{2}L_{i,j}^{0}}{\max_{i\in[p]}(x_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})}.

∎

E.3
Deterministic Setting

E.3.1
Layer-Wise Smooth Case

Theorem 14
.

Let Assumptions
1
,
6
and
7
hold. Let
\{X^{k}\}_{k=0}^{K-1}
,
K\geq 1
, be the iterates of
Algorithm

2
run with
\mathcal{C}_{i}^{k}\in\mathbb{B}(\alpha_{P})
,
\mathcal{C}_{i,j}^{k}\in\mathbb{B}_{\star}(\alpha_{D})
, and

\displaystyle 0\leq\gamma_{i}^{k}\equiv\gamma_{i}\leq\frac{1}{2L_{i}^{0}+\frac{4}{\alpha_{D}}\sqrt{12+\frac{66}{\alpha_{P}^{2}}}\tilde{L}_{i}^{0}},\qquad i=1,\ldots,p.

Then

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\gamma_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\gamma_{l}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]\leq\frac{1}{K}\frac{4\Psi^{0}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\gamma_{l}},

where

\displaystyle\Psi^{k}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle f(X^{k})-f^{\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{6\gamma_{i}}{\alpha_{D}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{66\gamma_{i}}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}\left(\frac{2}{\alpha_{P}}-1\right)(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}\left\|X_{i}^{k}-W_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}.

Remark 15
.

Theorem

3
follows as a corollary of the more general result above by setting
p=1
and initializing with
X^{0}=W^{0}
and
G_{j}^{0}=\nabla f_{j}(X^{0})
for all
j\in[n]
.

Remark 16
.

In the Euclidean case and when
p=1
, our convergence guarantees recover several existing results. When primal compression is disabled (i.e.,
\alpha_{P}=1
), they match the rate of
Richtárik et al. 2021
, up to constant factors. With primal compression, the rate coincides with that of
EF21-BC
in
Fatkhullin et al. 2021
. Additionally, our results match those of
Byz-EF21-BC
(a bidirectionally compressed method with error feedback for Byzantine-robust learning) from
Rammal et al. 2024
, in the absence of Byzantine workers.

Proof of
Theorem

14
.

Let
A_{i},B_{i}>0
be some constants to be determined later, and define

\displaystyle\Psi^{k}:=f(X^{k})-f^{\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}B_{i}\left\|X_{i}^{k}-W_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}.

Step I: Bounding
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]
.

The algorithm’s update rule gives

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right|X^{k+1},W^{k+1},G_{i,j}^{k}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k}-\mathcal{C}_{i,j}^{k}(\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right|X^{k+1},W^{k+1},G_{i,j}^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:young}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1+\frac{\alpha_{D}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k}-\mathcal{C}_{i,j}^{k}(\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right|X^{k+1},W^{k+1},G_{i,j}^{k}\right]

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{2}{\alpha_{D}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right|X^{k+1},W^{k+1},G_{i,j}^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1+\frac{\alpha_{D}}{2}\right)(1-\alpha_{D}){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right|X^{k+1},W^{k+1},G_{i,j}^{k}\right]

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{2}{\alpha_{D}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right|X^{k+1},W^{k+1},G_{i,j}^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:ineq1}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{D}}{2}\right)\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}+\left(1+\frac{2}{\alpha_{D}}\right)\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:young}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{D}}{2}\right)\left(1+\frac{\alpha_{D}}{4}\right)\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle+\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{D}}{2}\right)\left(1+\frac{4}{\alpha_{D}}\right)\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{2}{\alpha_{D}}\right)\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:ineq1},\eqref{eq:ineq2}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{D}}{4}\right)\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}+\frac{4}{\alpha_{D}}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{2}{\alpha_{D}}\right)\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(W^{k+1})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}.

Therefore, using smoothness,

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right|X^{k+1},W^{k+1},G_{i,j}^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{as:workers_layer_smoothness}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{D}}{4}\right)\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}+\frac{4}{\alpha_{D}}(L_{i,j}^{0})^{2}\left\|W_{i}^{k+1}-X_{i}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)}

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{2}{\alpha_{D}}\right)(L_{i,j}^{0})^{2}\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{(i)}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:young}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{D}}{4}\right)\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}+\frac{8}{\alpha_{D}}(L_{i,j}^{0})^{2}\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-X_{i}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)}

\displaystyle+\frac{8}{\alpha_{D}}(L_{i,j}^{0})^{2}\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{(i)}+\left(1+\frac{2}{\alpha_{D}}\right)(L_{i,j}^{0})^{2}\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{(i)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{D}}{4}\right)\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}+\frac{8}{\alpha_{D}}(L_{i,j}^{0})^{2}\gamma_{i}^{2}\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{11}{\alpha_{D}}(L_{i,j}^{0})^{2}\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{(i)}.

Taking expectation, we obtain the recursion

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]

(13)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{D}}{4}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]+\frac{8}{\alpha_{D}}(L_{i,j}^{0})^{2}\gamma_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{11}{\alpha_{D}}(L_{i,j}^{0})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{(i)}\right].

Step II: Bounding
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right]
.

By
Lemma

3

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right]\leq\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{P}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k}-W_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right]+\frac{2}{\alpha_{P}}\gamma_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right].

(14)

Step III: Bounding
\Psi^{k+1}
.

By
Lemma

1
and Jensen’s inequality

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91684pt\Psi^{k+1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f(X^{k+1})-f^{\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}B_{i}\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle f(X^{k})-f^{\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{3\gamma_{i}}{2}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-G_{i}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\gamma_{i}}{4}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{1}{4\gamma_{i}}-\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{2}\right)\gamma_{i}^{2}\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}B_{i}\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle f(X^{k})-f^{\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{3\gamma_{i}}{2}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\gamma_{i}}{4}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{1}{4\gamma_{i}}-\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{2}\right)\gamma_{i}^{2}\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}B_{i}\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}.

Taking expectation and using (
13
) gives

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\Psi^{k+1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[f(X^{k})-f^{\star}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{3\gamma_{i}}{2}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\gamma_{i}}{4}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{1}{4\gamma_{i}}-\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{2}\right)\gamma_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{D}}{4}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\frac{8}{\alpha_{D}}(L_{i,j}^{0})^{2}\gamma_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\frac{11}{\alpha_{D}}(L_{i,j}^{0})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{(i)}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{p}B_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[f(X^{k})-f^{\star}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{3\gamma_{i}}{2}+A_{i}\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{D}}{4}\right)\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\gamma_{i}}{4}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{1}{4\gamma_{i}}-\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{2}-A_{i}\frac{8}{\alpha_{D}}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}\right)\gamma_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(A_{i}\frac{11}{\alpha_{D}}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}+B_{i}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right].

Next, applying (
14
), we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\Psi^{k+1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[f(X^{k})-f^{\star}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{3\gamma_{i}}{2}+A_{i}\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{D}}{4}\right)\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\gamma_{i}}{4}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{1}{4\gamma_{i}}-\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{2}-A_{i}\frac{8}{\alpha_{D}}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}\right)\gamma_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(A_{i}\frac{11}{\alpha_{D}}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}+B_{i}\right)\frac{2}{\alpha_{P}}\gamma_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(A_{i}\frac{11}{\alpha_{D}}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}+B_{i}\right)\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{P}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k}-W_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right].

Taking
A_{i}=\frac{6\gamma_{i}}{\alpha_{D}}
and
B_{i}=A_{i}\frac{11}{\alpha_{D}}\left(\frac{2}{\alpha_{P}}-1\right)(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}=\frac{66\gamma_{i}}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}\left(\frac{2}{\alpha_{P}}-1\right)(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}
yields

\displaystyle\frac{3\gamma_{i}}{2}+A_{i}\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{D}}{4}\right)=A_{i},

\displaystyle\left(A_{i}\frac{11}{\alpha_{D}}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}+B_{i}\right)\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{P}}{2}\right)=B_{i},

and consequently,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\Psi^{k+1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[f(X^{k})-f^{\star}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\gamma_{i}}{4}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{1}{4\gamma_{i}}-\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{2}-\frac{8A_{i}}{\alpha_{D}}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}\right)\gamma_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{B_{i}}{1-\frac{\alpha_{P}}{2}}\frac{2}{\alpha_{P}}\gamma_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{p}B_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k}-W_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\Psi^{k}\right]-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\gamma_{i}}{4}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{1}{4\gamma_{i}}-\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{2}-\frac{8A_{i}}{\alpha_{D}}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}-\frac{4B_{i}}{\alpha_{P}(2-\alpha_{P})}\right)\gamma_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right].

Now, note that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4\gamma_{i}}-\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{2}-\frac{8A_{i}}{\alpha_{D}}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}-\frac{4B_{i}}{\alpha_{P}(2-\alpha_{P})}=\frac{1}{4\gamma_{i}}-\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{2}-\underbrace{\left(\frac{48}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}+\frac{264}{\alpha_{P}^{2}\alpha_{D}^{2}}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}\right)}_{:=\zeta_{i}}\gamma_{i}\geq 0

for
\gamma_{i}\leq\frac{1}{2L_{i}^{0}+2\sqrt{\zeta_{i}}}
. For such a choice of the stepsizes, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\Psi^{k+1}\right]\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\Psi^{k}\right]-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\gamma_{i}}{4}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right],

and hence

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\gamma_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]\leq 4\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\left({\mathbb{E}}\left[\Psi^{k}\right]-{\mathbb{E}}\left[\Psi^{k+1}\right]\right)\leq 4\Psi^{0}.

Lastly, dividing by
\frac{K}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\gamma_{l}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\gamma_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\gamma_{l}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]\leq\frac{1}{K}\frac{4\Psi^{0}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\gamma_{l}}.

∎

E.3.2
Layer-Wise
(L^{0},L^{1})
–Smooth Case

We now consider a deterministic variant of
EF21-Muon
(
Algorithm

2
) without primal compression, which iterates

\displaystyle X_{i}^{k+1}

\displaystyle={\rm LMO}_{\mathcal{B}(X_{i}^{k},t_{i}^{k})}\left(G_{i}^{k}\right),

\displaystyle G_{i,j}^{k+1}

\displaystyle=G_{i,j}^{k}+\mathcal{C}_{i,j}^{k}(\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k}),

\displaystyle G_{i}^{k+1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}G_{i,j}^{k+1}=G_{i}^{k}+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathcal{C}_{i,j}^{k}(\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k}).

This corresponds to using identity compressors on the server side.

Theorem 17
.

Let Assumptions
1
,
2
,
8
and
9
hold and let
\{X^{k}\}_{k=0}^{K-1}
,
K\geq 1
, be the iterates of
Algorithm

2
run with
\mathcal{C}_{i}^{k}\equiv\mathcal{I}
(the identity compressor),
\mathcal{C}_{i,j}^{k}\in\mathbb{B}_{\star}(\alpha_{D})
, and

\displaystyle t_{i}^{k}\equiv t_{i}=\frac{\eta_{i}}{\sqrt{K+1}},\qquad i=1,\ldots,p,

for some
\eta_{i}>0
.
Then,

\displaystyle\hskip-17.07164pt\min_{k=0,\ldots,K}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{i}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\exp\left(4\max_{i\in[p],j\in[n]}(\eta_{i}^{2}C_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})\right)}{\sqrt{K+1}\left(\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{i}\right)}\Psi^{0}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{K+1}\left(\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{i}\right)}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}4\max_{i\in[p]}(\eta_{i}^{2}C_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})\left(f^{\star}-f_{j}^{\star}\right)+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}^{2}C_{i}L_{i,j}^{0}}{L_{i,j}^{1}}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\eta_{i}^{2}D_{i}\right).

where
C_{i}:=\frac{L_{i}^{1}}{2}+\frac{2\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}L^{1}_{i,\max}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}
,
D_{i}:=\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{2}+\frac{2\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}
and

\displaystyle\Psi^{k}:=f(X^{k})-f^{\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{2t_{i}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}.

Remark 18
.

Theorem

4
follows as a corollary of the result in
Theorem

17
by setting
p=1
and initializing with
G_{j}^{0}=\nabla f_{j}(X^{0})
for all
j\in[n]
.

Proof.

Let
A_{i}>0
be some constants to be determined later, and define

\displaystyle\Psi^{k}:=f(X^{k})-f^{\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}.

By
Lemma

2
and Jensen’s inequality

\displaystyle\Psi^{k+1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f(X^{k+1})-f^{\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle f(X^{k})-f^{\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}2t_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{L^{0}_{i}+L^{1}_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}}{2}t_{i}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle f(X^{k})-f^{\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}2t_{i}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{L^{0}_{i}+L^{1}_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}}{2}t_{i}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)\star}.

Taking expectation conditioned on
[X^{k+1},X^{k},G^{k}]
and using
Lemma

6
gives

\displaystyle\hskip-17.07164pt{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\Psi^{k+1}\right|X^{k+1},X^{k},G^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle f(X^{k})-f^{\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}2t_{i}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{L^{0}_{i}+L^{1}_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}}{2}t_{i}^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-G_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)\star}\right|X^{k+1},X^{k},G^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{lemma:ng_rec_l0l1}}{\leq}

\displaystyle f(X^{k})-f^{\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}2t_{i}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{L^{0}_{i}+L^{1}_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}}{2}t_{i}^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}+\left(L^{0}_{i,j}+L^{1}_{i,j}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)t_{i}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f(X^{k})-f^{\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(2t_{i}+A_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{L^{1}_{i}t_{i}^{2}}{2}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}+\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}t_{i}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}L^{1}_{i,j}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}^{2}L^{0}_{i}}{2}+\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}t_{i}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}.

Now, letting
A_{i}=\frac{2t_{i}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}
, we have

\displaystyle 2t_{i}+A_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}=2t_{i}+\frac{2t_{i}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}=A_{i},

and consequently,

\displaystyle\hskip-17.07164pt{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\Psi^{k+1}\right|X^{k+1},X^{k},G^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle f(X^{k})-f^{\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{L^{1}_{i}t_{i}^{2}}{2}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}+\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}t_{i}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}L^{1}_{i,j}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}^{2}L^{0}_{i}}{2}+\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}t_{i}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Psi^{k}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{L^{1}_{i}t_{i}^{2}}{2}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{2\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}L^{1}_{i,\max}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}t_{i}^{2}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{t_{i}^{2}L^{0}_{i}}{2}+\frac{2\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}t_{i}^{2}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Psi^{k}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\underbrace{\left(\frac{L_{i}^{1}}{2}+\frac{2\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}L^{1}_{i,\max}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}\right)}_{:=C_{i}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}+\underbrace{\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{2}+\frac{2\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}}_{:=D_{i}}\right)t_{i}^{2}.

Taking
t_{i}=\frac{\eta_{i}}{\sqrt{K+1}}
for some
\eta_{i}>0
and using
Lemma

11
with
x_{i}=\eta_{i}^{2}C_{i}
, we get

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91692pt{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\Psi^{k+1}\right|X^{k+1},X^{k},G^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Psi^{k}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}+\frac{1}{K+1}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\eta_{i}^{2}C_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}D_{i}t_{i}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{lemma:layer_gen_smooth2}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\Psi^{k}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}D_{i}t_{i}^{2}+\frac{1}{K+1}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}4\max_{i\in[p]}(\eta_{i}^{2}C_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})\left(f_{j}(X^{k})-f^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{K+1}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}4\max_{i\in[p]}(\eta_{i}^{2}C_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})\left(f^{\star}-f_{j}^{\star}\right)+\frac{1}{K+1}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{p}\eta_{i}^{4}C_{i}^{2}L_{i,j}^{0}}{\max_{i\in[p]}(\eta_{i}^{2}C_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Psi^{k}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{K+1}}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\eta_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}+\frac{4}{K+1}\max_{i\in[p],j\in[n]}(\eta_{i}^{2}C_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(f_{j}(X^{k})-f^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{K+1}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}4\max_{i\in[p]}(\eta_{i}^{2}C_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})\left(f^{\star}-f_{j}^{\star}\right)+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}^{2}C_{i}L_{i,j}^{0}}{L_{i,j}^{1}}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\eta_{i}^{2}D_{i}\right).

Now, since
\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(f_{j}(X^{k})-f^{\star}\right)=f(X^{k})-f^{\star}\leq\Psi^{k}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91692pt{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\Psi^{k+1}\right|X^{k+1},X^{k},G^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1+\frac{4}{K+1}\max_{i\in[p],j\in[n]}(\eta_{i}^{2}C_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})\right)\Psi^{k}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{K+1}}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\eta_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{K+1}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}4\max_{i\in[p]}(\eta_{i}^{2}C_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})\left(f^{\star}-f_{j}^{\star}\right)+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}^{2}C_{i}L_{i,j}^{0}}{L_{i,j}^{1}}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\eta_{i}^{2}D_{i}\right).

Taking expectation,

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91692pt{\mathbb{E}}\left[\Psi^{k+1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1+\underbrace{\frac{4}{K+1}\max_{i\in[p],j\in[n]}(\eta_{i}^{2}C_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})}_{:=a_{1}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\Psi^{k}\right]-\frac{1}{\sqrt{K+1}}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\eta_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{1}{K+1}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}4\max_{i\in[p]}(\eta_{i}^{2}C_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})\left(f^{\star}-f_{j}^{\star}\right)+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}^{2}C_{i}L_{i,j}^{0}}{L_{i,j}^{1}}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\eta_{i}^{2}D_{i}\right)}_{:=a_{2}},

and hence, applying
Lemma

15
with
A^{k}={\mathbb{E}}\left[\Psi^{k}\right]
and
B_{i}^{k}=\frac{\eta_{i}}{\sqrt{K+1}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]
,

\displaystyle\hskip-17.07164pt\min_{k=0,\ldots,K}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}}{\sqrt{K+1}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\exp\left(\frac{4}{K+1}\max_{i\in[p],j\in[n]}(\eta_{i}^{2}C_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})(K+1)\right)}{(K+1)}\Psi^{0}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{K+1}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}4\max_{i\in[p]}(\eta_{i}^{2}C_{i}L_{i,j}^{1})\left(f^{\star}-f_{j}^{\star}\right)+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}^{2}C_{i}L_{i,j}^{0}}{L_{i,j}^{1}}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\eta_{i}^{2}D_{i}\right).

Dividing by
\frac{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{i}}{\sqrt{K+1}}
finishes the proof.
∎

E.4
Stochastic Setting

E.4.1
Layer-Wise Smooth Case

Theorem 19
.

Let Assumptions
1
,
6
,
7
and
10
hold. Let
\{X^{k}\}_{k=0}^{K-1}
,
K\geq 1
, be the iterates of
Algorithm

3
run with
\mathcal{C}_{i}^{k}\in\mathbb{B}(\alpha_{P})
,
\mathcal{C}_{i,j}^{k}\in\mathbb{B}_{2}(\alpha_{D})
, any
\beta_{i}\in(0,1]
, and

\displaystyle 0\leq\gamma_{i}^{k}\equiv\gamma_{i}\leq\frac{1}{2L_{i}^{0}+2\sqrt{\zeta_{i}}},\qquad i=1,\ldots,p,

where
\zeta_{i}:=\frac{\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(\frac{12}{\beta_{i}^{2}}(L_{i}^{0})^{2}+\frac{24\left(\beta_{i}+2\right)}{\alpha_{P}^{2}}(L_{i}^{0})^{2}+\frac{36\left(\beta_{i}^{2}+4\right)}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}+\frac{144\beta_{i}^{2}\left(2\beta_{i}+5\right)}{\alpha_{P}^{2}\alpha_{D}^{2}}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}\right)
. Then

\displaystyle\hskip-17.07182pt\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\gamma_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\gamma_{l}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]

(15)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K}\frac{4\Psi^{0}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\gamma_{l}}+24\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{1}{n}+\frac{(1-\alpha_{D})\beta_{i}}{\alpha_{D}}+\frac{12\beta_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}\right)\frac{\sigma_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}\beta_{i}\gamma_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\gamma_{l}},

where

\displaystyle\Psi^{0}:=

\displaystyle\,f(X^{0})-f^{\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{6\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\beta_{i}}\gamma_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{0})-M_{i}^{0}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{72\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}\beta_{i}}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}\gamma_{i}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0})-M_{i,j}^{0}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{6\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}}\gamma_{i}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{0}-G_{i,j}^{0}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

and
M_{i}^{k}:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}M_{i,j}^{k}
.

Corollary 1
.

Let the assumptions of
Theorem

19
hold and let
\{X^{k}\}_{k=0}^{K-1}
,
K\geq 1
, be the iterates of
Algorithm

3
initialized with
M_{i,j}^{0}=G_{i,j}^{0}=\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0};\xi_{j}^{0})
,
j\in[n]
. Then, the result in
Theorem

19
guarantees that

\displaystyle\hskip-17.07182pt\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\gamma_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\gamma_{l}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4\left(f(X^{0})-f^{\star}\right)}{K\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\gamma_{l}}+\frac{24}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}\beta_{i}}+\frac{12\beta_{i}}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}\right)\frac{\sigma_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}\gamma_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\gamma_{l}}

\displaystyle+24\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{1}{n}+\frac{(1-\alpha_{D})\beta_{i}}{\alpha_{D}}+\frac{12\beta_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}\right)\frac{\sigma_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}\beta_{i}\gamma_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\gamma_{l}}.

Remark 20
.

Theorem

5
follows as a corollary of the result above by setting
p=1
.

Corollary 2
.

Let the assumptions of
Theorem

19
hold and let
\{X^{k}\}_{k=0}^{K-1}
,
K\geq 1
, be the iterates of
Algorithm

1
(
Algorithm

3
with
p=1
) run with
\mathcal{C}_{i}^{k}\in\mathbb{B}(\alpha_{P})
,
\mathcal{C}_{i,j}^{k}\in\mathbb{B}_{2}(\alpha_{D})
. Choosing the stepsize

\displaystyle\gamma_{1}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\zeta_{1}}+2L_{1}^{0}}=\mathcal{O}\left(\left(\frac{\bar{\rho}_{1}^{2}L_{1}^{0}}{\underline{\rho}_{1}^{2}\beta_{1}}+\frac{\bar{\rho}_{1}^{2}\tilde{L}_{1}^{0}}{\underline{\rho}_{1}^{2}\alpha_{P}\alpha_{D}}\right)^{-1}\right)

(16)

and momentum

\displaystyle\beta_{1}=\min\left\{1,\left(\frac{\Psi^{0}L_{1}^{0}n}{\underline{\rho}_{1}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{2}K}\right)^{1/2},\left(\frac{\Psi^{0}L_{1}^{0}\alpha_{D}}{\underline{\rho}_{1}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{2}K}\right)^{1/3},\left(\frac{\Psi^{0}L_{1}^{0}\alpha_{D}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{1}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{2}K}\right)^{1/4}\right\},

(17)

the result in
Theorem

19
guarantees that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla f(X^{k})\right\|_{\star}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\Psi^{0}\bar{\rho}_{1}^{2}\tilde{L}_{1}^{0}}{\underline{\rho}_{1}^{2}\alpha_{P}\alpha_{D}K}+\left(\frac{\Psi^{0}\bar{\rho}_{1}^{4}\sigma_{1}^{2}L_{1}^{0}}{\underline{\rho}_{1}^{2}nK}\right)^{1/2}+\left(\frac{\Psi^{0}\bar{\rho}_{1}^{3}\sigma_{1}L_{1}^{0}}{\underline{\rho}_{1}^{2}\sqrt{\alpha_{D}}K}\right)^{2/3}+\left(\frac{\Psi^{0}\underline{\rho}_{1}^{8/3}\sigma_{1}^{2/3}L_{1}^{0}}{\bar{\rho}_{1}^{2}\alpha_{D}^{2/3}K}\right)^{3/4}\right).

Remark 21
.

In the Euclidean case (
\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}=\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}=1
), without primal compression (
\alpha_{P}=1
), and for
p=1
, the result in
Theorem

19
simplifies to

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}\right]=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\Psi^{0}}{K\gamma}+\left(\frac{1}{n}+\frac{\beta}{\alpha_{D}}+\frac{\beta^{2}}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}\right)\beta\sigma^{2}\right),

for
\gamma=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\beta}{L_{1}^{0}}+\frac{\alpha_{D}}{\tilde{L}_{1}^{0}}\right)
, which recovers the rate of
EF21-SDGM
in
Fatkhullin et al. 2023
(up to a constant).

Remark 22
.

In the absence of stochasticity and momentum, i.e., when
\sigma_{i}^{2}=0
and
\beta_{i}=1
, and under the initialization
W^{0}=X^{0}
,
M_{j}^{0}=G_{j}^{0}=\nabla f_{j}(X^{0})
,
Algorithm

3
reduces to
Algorithm

2
. In this setting,
Theorem

19
guarantees that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\gamma_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\gamma_{l}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]\leq\frac{1}{K}\frac{4\left(f(X^{0})-f^{\star}\right)}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\gamma_{l}},

for

\displaystyle 0\leq\gamma_{i}^{k}\equiv\gamma_{i}\leq\frac{1}{2L_{i}^{0}+2\sqrt{\zeta_{i}}},\qquad i=1,\ldots,p,

where
\zeta_{i}:=\frac{\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(12(L_{i}^{0})^{2}+\frac{72}{\alpha_{P}^{2}}(L_{i}^{0})^{2}+\frac{180}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}+\frac{1008}{\alpha_{P}^{2}\alpha_{D}^{2}}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}\right)
. This recovers the guarantee in
Theorem

14
, up to a constant factor.

Remark 23
.

Alternatively, one may use compressors
\mathcal{C}_{i}^{k}\in\mathbb{B}_{2}(\alpha_{P})
in
Theorem

19
. The proof is essentially the same, with the only modification being the replacement of
Lemma

3
by the recursion

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt{\mathbb{E}}_{\mathcal{C}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\mathcal{C}}\left[\left\|W_{i}^{k}+\mathcal{C}_{i}^{k}(X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k})-X_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\alpha_{P})\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:young}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1-\alpha_{P})\left(1+\frac{\alpha_{P}}{2}\right)\left\|X_{i}^{k}-W_{i}^{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}+(1-\alpha_{P})\left(1+\frac{2}{\alpha_{P}}\right)\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-X_{i}^{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:ineq1},\eqref{eq:ineq2}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{P}}{2}\right)\left\|X_{i}^{k}-W_{i}^{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{2\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{P}}\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-X_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{P}}{2}\right)\left\|X_{i}^{k}-W_{i}^{k}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{2\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{P}}(\gamma_{i}^{k})^{2}\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}.

The resulting convergence guarantee matches that of
Theorem

19
up to a modification of the constant
\zeta_{i}
, which now becomes

\displaystyle\zeta_{i}=\frac{\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(\frac{12}{\beta_{i}^{2}}(L_{i}^{0})^{2}+\frac{24{\color[rgb]{0.8633,0.0781,0.2344}\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(\beta_{i}+2\right)}{\alpha_{P}^{2}}(L_{i}^{0})^{2}+\frac{36\left(\beta_{i}^{2}+4\right)}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}+\frac{144{\color[rgb]{0.8633,0.0781,0.2344}\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\beta_{i}^{2}\left(2\beta_{i}+5\right)}{\alpha_{P}^{2}\alpha_{D}^{2}}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}\right),

where the additional norm equivalence factors highlighted in
red
arise due to the use of Euclidean compressors.

Proof of
Theorem

19
.

Lemma

1
and Young’s and Jensen’s inequalities give

\displaystyle f(X^{k+1})

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{lemma:descent1}}{\leq}

\displaystyle f(X^{k})+\frac{3}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\gamma_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-G_{i}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\gamma_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{1}{4\gamma_{i}}-\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{2}\right)\gamma_{i}^{2}\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:young}}{\leq}

\displaystyle f(X^{k})+3\sum_{i=1}^{p}\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}\gamma_{i}\left(\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-M_{i}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\|M_{i,j}^{k}-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\gamma_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{1}{4\gamma_{i}}-\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{2}\right)\gamma_{i}^{2}\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}.

Recall that by Lemmas
3
,
4
and
5
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{lemma:xw_sq_rec_sharp}}{\leq}\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{P}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k}-W_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right]+\frac{2}{\alpha_{P}}\gamma_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right],

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{k+1}-G_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{lemma:mg_sq_rec_m_sharp2}}{\leq}\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{D}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{k}-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]+\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{k}-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}(L_{i,j}^{0})^{2}}{\alpha_{D}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\gamma_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{\star}^{2}\right]+\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}(L_{i,j}^{0})^{2}}{\alpha_{D}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+(1-\alpha_{D})\beta_{i}^{2}\sigma_{i}^{2},

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k+1})-M_{i,j}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{lemma:nm_sq_rec_m_sharp2}}{\leq}\left(1-\frac{\beta_{i}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-M_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]+\frac{2(L_{i,j}^{0})^{2}}{\beta_{i}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\gamma_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\beta_{i}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right)(L_{i,j}^{0})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right]+\beta_{i}^{2}\sigma_{i}^{2},

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k+1})-M_{i}^{k+1}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]
