\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{lemma:nm_sq_rec_m_sharp2}}{\leq}\left(1-\frac{\beta_{i}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-M_{i}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]+\frac{2(L_{i}^{0})^{2}}{\beta_{i}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\gamma_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\beta_{i}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right)(L_{i}^{0})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k+1}-W_{i}^{k+1}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right]+\frac{\beta_{i}^{2}\sigma_{i}^{2}}{n},

where
M_{i}^{k}:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}M_{i,j}^{k}
.
To simplify the notation, let us define
\delta^{k}:={\mathbb{E}}\left[f(X^{k})-f^{\star}\right]
,

P_{i}^{k}:=\gamma_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-M_{i}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]
,

\tilde{P}_{i}^{k}:=\gamma_{i}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-M_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]
,

\tilde{S}_{i}^{k}:=\gamma_{i}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{k}-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

and
R_{i}^{k}:=\gamma_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X_{i}^{k}-W_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}\right]
. Then, the above inequalities yield

\displaystyle\delta^{k+1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{k}+3\sum_{i=1}^{p}\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}P_{i}^{k}+3\sum_{i=1}^{p}\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}\tilde{S}_{i}^{k}-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\gamma_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]

(18)

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{1}{4\gamma_{i}}-\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{2}\right)\gamma_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right],

\displaystyle R_{i}^{k+1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{P}}{2}\right)R_{i}^{k}+\frac{2}{\alpha_{P}}\gamma_{i}^{3}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right],

(19)

\displaystyle\tilde{S}_{i}^{k+1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{D}}{2}\right)\tilde{S}_{i}^{k}+\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}}\tilde{P}_{i}^{k}+\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}}{\alpha_{D}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\gamma_{i}^{3}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]

(20)

\displaystyle+\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}}{\alpha_{D}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}R_{i}^{k+1}+(1-\alpha_{D})\sigma_{i}^{2}\beta_{i}^{2}\gamma_{i},

\displaystyle\tilde{P}_{i}^{k+1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\beta_{i}}{2}\right)\tilde{P}_{i}^{k}+\frac{2(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}}{\beta_{i}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\gamma_{i}^{3}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]

(21)

\displaystyle+\frac{\beta_{i}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right)(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}R_{i}^{k+1}+\sigma_{i}^{2}\beta_{i}^{2}\gamma_{i},

\displaystyle P_{i}^{k+1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\beta_{i}}{2}\right)P_{i}^{k}+\frac{2(L_{i}^{0})^{2}}{\beta_{i}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\gamma_{i}^{3}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]

(22)

\displaystyle+\frac{\beta_{i}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right)(L_{i}^{0})^{2}R_{i}^{k+1}+\frac{\sigma_{i}^{2}\beta_{i}^{2}\gamma_{i}}{n}.

Now, let
A_{i},B_{i},C_{i},D_{i}>0
be some constants to be determined later, and define

\displaystyle\Psi^{k}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\delta^{k}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}P_{i}^{k}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}B_{i}\tilde{P}_{i}^{k}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}C_{i}\tilde{S}_{i}^{k}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}D_{i}R_{i}^{k}.

Then, applying (
18
), (
20
), (
21
), and (
22
), we have

\displaystyle\hskip-17.07182pt\Psi^{k+1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta^{k+1}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}P_{i}^{k+1}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}B_{i}\tilde{P}_{i}^{k+1}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}C_{i}\tilde{S}_{i}^{k+1}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}D_{i}R_{i}^{k+1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{k}+3\sum_{i=1}^{p}\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}P_{i}^{k}+3\sum_{i=1}^{p}\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}\tilde{S}_{i}^{k}-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\gamma_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{1}{4\gamma_{i}}-\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{2}\right)\gamma_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}\left(\left(1-\frac{\beta_{i}}{2}\right)P_{i}^{k}+\frac{2(L_{i}^{0})^{2}}{\beta_{i}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\gamma_{i}^{3}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]+\frac{\beta_{i}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right)(L_{i}^{0})^{2}R_{i}^{k+1}+\frac{\sigma_{i}^{2}\beta_{i}^{2}\gamma_{i}}{n}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}B_{i}\left(\left(1-\frac{\beta_{i}}{2}\right)\tilde{P}_{i}^{k}+\frac{2(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}}{\beta_{i}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\gamma_{i}^{3}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]+\frac{\beta_{i}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right)(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}R_{i}^{k+1}+\sigma_{i}^{2}\beta_{i}^{2}\gamma_{i}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}C_{i}\left(\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{D}}{2}\right)\tilde{S}_{i}^{k}+\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}}\tilde{P}_{i}^{k}+\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}}{\alpha_{D}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\gamma_{i}^{3}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}C_{i}\left(\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}}{\alpha_{D}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}R_{i}^{k+1}+(1-\alpha_{D})\sigma_{i}^{2}\beta_{i}^{2}\gamma_{i}\right)+\sum_{i=1}^{p}D_{i}R_{i}^{k+1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta^{k}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(3\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}+A_{i}\left(1-\frac{\beta_{i}}{2}\right)\right)P_{i}^{k}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(B_{i}\left(1-\frac{\beta_{i}}{2}\right)+C_{i}\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}}\right)\tilde{P}_{i}^{k}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(3\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}+C_{i}\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{D}}{2}\right)\right)\tilde{S}_{i}^{k}-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\gamma_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(A_{i}\frac{\beta_{i}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right)(L_{i}^{0})^{2}+B_{i}\frac{\beta_{i}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right)(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}+C_{i}\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}}{\alpha_{D}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}+D_{i}\right)R_{i}^{k+1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(A_{i}\frac{2(L_{i}^{0})^{2}}{\beta_{i}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}+B_{i}\frac{2(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}}{\beta_{i}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}+C_{i}\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}}{\alpha_{D}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\right)\gamma_{i}^{3}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{1}{4\gamma_{i}}-\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{2}\right)\gamma_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{A_{i}}{n}+B_{i}+C_{i}(1-\alpha_{D})\right)\sigma_{i}^{2}\beta_{i}^{2}\gamma_{i}.

Then, using (
19
) gives

\displaystyle\Psi^{k+1}

\displaystyle\leq\delta^{k}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(3\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}+A_{i}\left(1-\frac{\beta_{i}}{2}\right)\right)P_{i}^{k}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(B_{i}\left(1-\frac{\beta_{i}}{2}\right)+C_{i}\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}}\right)\tilde{P}_{i}^{k}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(3\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}+C_{i}\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{D}}{2}\right)\right)\tilde{S}_{i}^{k}-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\gamma_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(A_{i}\frac{\beta_{i}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right)(L_{i}^{0})^{2}+B_{i}\frac{\beta_{i}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right)(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}+C_{i}\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}}{\alpha_{D}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}+D_{i}\right)\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{P}}{2}\right)R_{i}^{k}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(A_{i}\frac{\beta_{i}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right)(L_{i}^{0})^{2}+B_{i}\frac{\beta_{i}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right)(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}+C_{i}\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}}{\alpha_{D}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}+D_{i}\right)\frac{2}{\alpha_{P}}\gamma_{i}^{3}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(A_{i}\frac{2(L_{i}^{0})^{2}}{\beta_{i}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}+B_{i}\frac{2(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}}{\beta_{i}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}+C_{i}\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}}{\alpha_{D}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\right)\gamma_{i}^{3}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{1}{4\gamma_{i}}-\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{2}\right)\gamma_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{A_{i}}{n}+B_{i}+C_{i}(1-\alpha_{D})\right)\sigma_{i}^{2}\beta_{i}^{2}\gamma_{i}.

Taking
A_{i}=\frac{6\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\beta_{i}}
,
B_{i}=\frac{72\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}\beta_{i}}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}
,
C_{i}=\frac{6\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}}
and

\displaystyle D_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(A_{i}\frac{\beta_{i}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right)(L_{i}^{0})^{2}+B_{i}\frac{\beta_{i}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right)(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}+C_{i}\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}}{\alpha_{D}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{2}{\alpha_{P}}-1\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{6\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(\left(\beta_{i}+2\right)(L_{i}^{0})^{2}+\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}\left(2\beta_{i}+5\right)}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}\right)\left(\frac{2}{\alpha_{P}}-1\right),

we obtain

\displaystyle 3\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}+A_{i}\left(1-\frac{\beta_{i}}{2}\right)=3\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}+\frac{6\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\beta_{i}}\left(1-\frac{\beta_{i}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle=A_{i},

\displaystyle B_{i}\left(1-\frac{\beta_{i}}{2}\right)+C_{i}\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}}=\frac{72\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}\beta_{i}}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}\left(1-\frac{\beta_{i}}{2}\right)+\frac{6\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}}\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}}

\displaystyle=B_{i},

\displaystyle 3\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}+C_{i}\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{D}}{2}\right)=3\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}+\frac{6\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}}\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{D}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle=C_{i},

and

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91684pt\left(A_{i}\frac{\beta_{i}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right)(L_{i}^{0})^{2}+B_{i}\frac{\beta_{i}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta_{i}}\right)(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}+C_{i}\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}}{\alpha_{D}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}+D_{i}\right)\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{P}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\frac{D_{i}}{\frac{2}{\alpha_{P}}-1}+D_{i}\right)\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{P}}{2}\right)=D_{i}.

Consequently,

\displaystyle\hskip-17.07182pt\Psi^{k+1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{k}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}P_{i}^{k}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}B_{i}\tilde{P}_{i}^{k}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}C_{i}\tilde{S}_{i}^{k}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}D_{i}R_{i}^{k}-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\gamma_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{D_{i}}{\frac{2}{\alpha_{P}}-1}+D_{i}\right)\frac{2}{\alpha_{P}}\gamma_{i}^{3}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{1}{4\gamma_{i}}-\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{2}\right)\gamma_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{6\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\beta_{i}}\frac{2(L_{i}^{0})^{2}}{\beta_{i}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}+\frac{72\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}\beta_{i}}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}\frac{2(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}}{\beta_{i}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}+\frac{6\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}}\frac{6\beta_{i}^{2}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}}{\alpha_{D}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\right)\gamma_{i}^{3}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{1}{n}\frac{6\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\beta_{i}}+\frac{72\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}\beta_{i}}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}+\frac{6\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}}(1-\alpha_{D})\right)\sigma_{i}^{2}\beta_{i}^{2}\gamma_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Psi^{k}-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\gamma_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{1}{4\gamma_{i}}-\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{2}\right)\gamma_{i}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{12\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\beta_{i}^{2}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}(L_{i}^{0})^{2}+\frac{144\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}^{2}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}+\frac{36\beta_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}^{2}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}+\frac{4D_{i}}{\alpha_{P}(2-\alpha_{P})}\right)\gamma_{i}^{3}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+6\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{1}{n}+\frac{12\beta_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}+\frac{(1-\alpha_{D})\beta_{i}}{\alpha_{D}}\right)\sigma_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}\beta_{i}\gamma_{i}.

Now, note that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4\gamma_{i}}-\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{2}-\gamma_{i}\left(\frac{12\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\beta_{i}^{2}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}(L_{i}^{0})^{2}+\frac{144\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}^{2}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}+\frac{36\beta_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}^{2}\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}+\frac{4D_{i}}{\alpha_{P}(2-\alpha_{P})}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4\gamma_{i}}-\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\gamma_{i}\underbrace{\frac{\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}^{2}}\left(\frac{12}{\beta_{i}^{2}}(L_{i}^{0})^{2}+\frac{24\left(\beta_{i}+2\right)}{\alpha_{P}^{2}}(L_{i}^{0})^{2}+\frac{36\left(\beta_{i}^{2}+4\right)}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}+\frac{144\beta_{i}^{2}\left(2\beta_{i}+5\right)}{\alpha_{P}^{2}\alpha_{D}^{2}}(\tilde{L}_{i}^{0})^{2}\right)}_{:=\zeta_{i}}\geq 0

for
\gamma_{i}\leq\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\zeta_{i}}+2L_{i}^{0}}
. For such a choice of the stepsizes, we have

\displaystyle\Psi^{k+1}\leq\Psi^{k}-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\gamma_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\underbrace{6\left(\frac{1}{n}+\frac{12\beta_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}+\frac{(1-\alpha_{D})\beta_{i}}{\alpha_{D}}\right)\sigma_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}\beta_{i}}_{:=\xi_{i}}\gamma_{i}.

Summing over the first
K
iterations gives

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\gamma_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]\leq 4\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\left(\Psi^{k}-\Psi^{k+1}\right)+4\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\xi_{i}\gamma_{i}\leq 4\Psi^{0}+4K\sum_{i=1}^{p}\xi_{i}\gamma_{i},

and lastly, dividing by
\frac{K}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\gamma_{l}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\gamma_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\gamma_{l}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]\leq\frac{4\Psi^{0}p}{K\sum_{l=1}^{p}\gamma_{l}}+\frac{4\sum_{i=1}^{p}\xi_{i}\gamma_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\gamma_{i}}.

Substituting
X_{i}^{0}=W_{i}^{0}
proves the theorem statement.
∎

Proof of
Corollary

1
.

Substituting the initialization, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{0})-M_{i}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0};\xi_{j}^{0})\right)\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0};\xi_{j}^{0})\right)\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]}\overset{\eqref{as:bounded_layer_var}}{\leq}\frac{\sigma_{i}}{\sqrt{n}},

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0})-M_{i,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0};\xi_{j}^{0})\right\|_{2}\right]\overset{\eqref{as:bounded_layer_var}}{\leq}\sigma_{i},

and hence

\displaystyle\Psi^{0}

\displaystyle:=f(X^{0})-f^{\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{6\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\beta_{i}}\gamma_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{0})-M_{i}^{0}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{72\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}\beta_{i}}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}\gamma_{i}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0})-M_{i,j}^{0}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{6\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}}\gamma_{i}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{0}-G_{i,j}^{0}\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq f(X^{0})-f^{\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{6\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}}{\sqrt{n}\beta_{i}}\gamma_{i}\sigma_{i}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{72\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}\beta_{i}}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}\gamma_{i}\sigma_{i}.

Substituting this in the rate, we get

\displaystyle\hskip-17.07182pt\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\gamma_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\gamma_{l}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|^{2}_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4\left(f(X^{0})-f^{\star}\right)}{K\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\gamma_{l}}+\frac{24}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}\beta_{i}}+\frac{12\beta_{i}}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}\right)\frac{\sigma_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}\gamma_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\gamma_{l}}

\displaystyle+24\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{1}{n}+\frac{(1-\alpha_{D})\beta_{i}}{\alpha_{D}}+\frac{12\beta_{i}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}\right)\frac{\sigma_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}^{2}\beta_{i}\gamma_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\gamma_{l}}.

∎

Proof of
Corollary

2
.

Substituting the choice of
\gamma
from (
16
) in (
15
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla f(X^{k})\right\|_{\star}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\Psi^{0}}{K\gamma_{1}}+24\left(\frac{1}{n}+\frac{(1-\alpha_{D})\beta_{1}}{\alpha_{D}}+\frac{12\beta_{1}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}\right)\sigma_{1}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{1}^{2}\beta_{1}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\Psi^{0}\bar{\rho}_{1}^{2}\tilde{L}_{1}^{0}}{\underline{\rho}_{1}^{2}\alpha_{P}\alpha_{D}K}+\frac{\Psi^{0}\bar{\rho}_{1}^{2}L_{1}^{0}}{\underline{\rho}_{1}^{2}\beta_{1}K}+\frac{\bar{\rho}_{1}^{2}\beta_{1}\sigma_{1}^{2}}{n}+\frac{\bar{\rho}_{1}^{2}\beta_{1}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}}+\frac{\bar{\rho}_{1}^{2}\beta_{1}^{3}\sigma_{1}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}\right).

Then, choosing
\beta_{1}
as in (
17
) guarantees that
\frac{\bar{\rho}_{1}^{2}\beta_{1}\sigma_{1}^{2}}{n},\frac{\bar{\rho}_{1}^{2}\beta_{1}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}},\frac{\bar{\rho}_{1}^{2}\beta_{1}^{3}\sigma_{1}^{2}}{\alpha_{D}^{2}}\leq\frac{\Psi^{0}\bar{\rho}_{1}^{2}L_{1}^{0}}{\underline{\rho}_{1}^{2}\beta_{1}K}
. Substituting this into the upper bound gives

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla f(X^{k})\right\|_{\star}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\Psi^{0}\bar{\rho}_{1}^{2}\tilde{L}_{1}^{0}}{\underline{\rho}_{1}^{2}\alpha_{P}\alpha_{D}K}+\left(\frac{\Psi^{0}\bar{\rho}_{1}^{4}\sigma_{1}^{2}L_{1}^{0}}{\underline{\rho}_{1}^{2}nK}\right)^{1/2}+\left(\frac{\Psi^{0}\bar{\rho}_{1}^{3}\sigma_{1}L_{1}^{0}}{\underline{\rho}_{1}^{2}\sqrt{\alpha_{D}}K}\right)^{2/3}+\left(\frac{\Psi^{0}\underline{\rho}_{1}^{8/3}\sigma_{1}^{2/3}L_{1}^{0}}{\bar{\rho}_{1}^{2}\alpha_{D}^{2/3}K}\right)^{3/4}\right)

as needed.
∎

E.4.2
Layer-Wise
(L^{0},L^{1})
–Smooth Case

As in

, in the generalized smooth setting we consider
EF21-Muon
without primal compression.

Theorem 24
.

Let Assumptions
1
,
2
,
8
,
9
and
10
hold. Let
\{X^{k}\}_{k=0}^{K-1}
,
K\geq 1
, be the iterates of
Algorithm

3
run with
\mathcal{C}_{i}^{k}\equiv\mathcal{I}
(the identity compressor),
\mathcal{C}_{i,j}^{k}\in\mathbb{B}_{2}(\alpha_{D})
,
\beta_{i}\equiv\beta=\frac{1}{(K+1)^{1/2}}
and

\displaystyle 0\leq t_{i}^{k}\equiv t_{i}=\frac{\eta_{i}}{(K+1)^{3/4}},\qquad i=1,\ldots,p,

where
\eta_{i}^{2}\leq\min\left\{\frac{(K+1)^{1/2}}{6(L^{1}_{i})^{2}},\frac{(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})\underline{\rho}_{i}(K+1)^{1/2}}{24(K+1)\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\bar{\rho}_{i}(L^{1}_{i,\max})^{2}},\frac{\beta_{\min}\underline{\rho}_{i}(K+1)^{1/2}}{24\bar{\rho}_{i}(L_{i,\max}^{1})^{2}},1\right\}
.
Then

\displaystyle\hskip-22.76219pt\min_{k=0,\ldots,K}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{3\Psi^{0}}{(K+1)^{1/4}\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}+\frac{6}{(K+1)^{1/2}}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{0})-M_{i}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{8}{(K+1)^{1/4}}+\frac{8\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})(K+1)^{3/4}}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\frac{\max_{i\in[p]}\eta_{i}^{2}\frac{\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}(L_{i,j}^{1})^{2}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}\left(f^{\star}-f_{j}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}^{2}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}\left(\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{(K+1)^{3/4}}+\frac{4\bar{\rho}_{i}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}(K+1)^{1/4}}+\frac{4\bar{\rho}_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})(K+1)^{3/4}}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sigma_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}\left(\frac{4\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})(K+1)^{1/2}}+\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}(K+1)^{1/4}}\right),

where
M_{i}^{0}:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}M_{i,j}^{0}
and

\displaystyle\Psi^{0}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle f(X^{0})-f^{\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{0}-G_{i,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0})-M_{i,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right].

Corollary 3
.

Let the assumptions of
Theorem

24
hold and let
\{X^{k}\}_{k=0}^{K-1}
,
K\geq 1
, be the iterates of
Algorithm

3
initialized with
M_{i,j}^{0}=\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0};\xi_{j}^{0})
,
G_{i,j}^{0}=\mathcal{C}_{i,j}^{0}(\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0};\xi_{j}^{0}))
,
j\in[n]
, and run with
\mathcal{C}_{i}^{k}\equiv\mathcal{I}
(the identity compressor),
\mathcal{C}_{i,j}^{k}\in\mathbb{B}_{2}(\alpha_{D})
,
\beta_{i}\equiv\beta=\frac{1}{(K+1)^{1/2}}
and

\displaystyle 0\leq t_{i}^{k}\equiv t_{i}=\frac{\eta_{i}}{(K+1)^{3/4}},\qquad i=1,\ldots,p,

where
\eta_{i}^{2}\leq\min\left\{\frac{(K+1)^{1/2}}{6(L^{1}_{i})^{2}},\frac{(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})\underline{\rho}_{i}(K+1)^{1/2}}{24(K+1)\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\bar{\rho}_{i}(L^{1}_{i,\max})^{2}},\frac{\beta_{\min}\underline{\rho}_{i}(K+1)^{1/2}}{24\bar{\rho}_{i}(L_{i,\max}^{1})^{2}},1\right\}
.
Then, the result in
Theorem

19
guarantees that

\displaystyle\hskip-22.76219pt\min_{k=0,\ldots,K}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{3}{(K+1)^{1/4}\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}\left(f(X^{0})-f^{\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{4\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\eta_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sigma_{i}}{(K+1)^{3/4}(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{6}{(K+1)^{1/2}}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\bar{\rho}_{i}\eta_{i}\sigma_{i}}{\sqrt{n}\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{8}{(K+1)^{1/4}}+\frac{8\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{(K+1)^{3/4}(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\frac{\max_{i\in[p]}\eta_{i}^{2}\frac{\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}(L_{i,j}^{1})^{2}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}\left(f^{\star}-f_{j}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}^{2}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}\left(\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{(K+1)^{3/4}}+\frac{4\bar{\rho}_{i}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}(K+1)^{1/4}}+\frac{4\bar{\rho}_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}(K+1)^{3/4}(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sigma_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}\left(\frac{4\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{(K+1)^{1/2}(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})}+\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}(K+1)^{1/4}}\right).

Remark 25
.

Theorem

6
follows from
Corollary

3
by setting
p=1
:

\displaystyle\hskip-22.76219pt\min_{k=0,\ldots,K}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla f(X^{k})\right\|_{\star}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{3\left(f(X^{0})-f^{\star}\right)}{\eta(K+1)^{1/4}}+\frac{12\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\bar{\rho}\sigma}{(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})(K+1)}+\frac{6\bar{\rho}\sigma}{\sqrt{n}(K+1)^{1/2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta\bar{\rho}}{\underline{\rho}}\left(\frac{8}{(K+1)^{1/4}}+\frac{8\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})(K+1)^{3/4}}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}(L_{j}^{1})^{2}\left(f^{\star}-f_{j}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta L^{0}}{(K+1)^{3/4}}+\frac{\eta\bar{\rho}}{\underline{\rho}}\left(\frac{4}{(K+1)^{1/4}}+\frac{4\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})(K+1)^{3/4}}\right)\bar{L}^{0}

\displaystyle+\frac{4\bar{\rho}\sigma\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})(K+1)^{1/2}}+\frac{2\bar{\rho}\sigma}{\sqrt{n}(K+1)^{1/4}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{3\left(f(X^{0})-f^{\star}\right)}{\eta(K+1)^{1/4}}+\frac{16\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\bar{\rho}\sigma}{(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})(K+1)^{1/2}}+\frac{\eta L^{0}}{(K+1)^{3/4}}+\frac{8\bar{\rho}\sigma}{\sqrt{n}(K+1)^{1/4}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta\bar{\rho}}{\underline{\rho}}\left(\frac{8}{(K+1)^{1/4}}+\frac{8\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})(K+1)^{3/4}}\right)\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}(L_{j}^{1})^{2}\left(f^{\star}-f_{j}^{\star}\right)+\bar{L}^{0}\right).

Proof of
Theorem

24
.

By
Lemma

2
and Jensen’s inequality

\displaystyle f(X^{k+1})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle f(X^{k})+\sum_{i=1}^{p}2t_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{L^{0}_{i}+L^{1}_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}}{2}t_{i}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle f(X^{k})+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(2t_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-M_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}+2t_{i}\left\|M_{i}^{k}-G_{i}^{k}\right\|_{(i)\star}\right)

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{L^{0}_{i}}{2}t_{i}^{2}+\frac{L^{1}_{i}}{2}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}t_{i}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle f(X^{k})+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(2\bar{\rho}_{i}t_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-M_{i}^{k}\right\|_{2}+2\bar{\rho}_{i}t_{i}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{k}-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|_{2}\right]\right)

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{L^{0}_{i}}{2}t_{i}^{2}+\frac{L^{1}_{i}}{2}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}t_{i}^{2}\right).

To simplify the notation, let
\delta^{k}:={\mathbb{E}}\left[f(X^{k})-f^{\star}\right]
,

P_{i}^{k}:={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})-M_{i}^{k}\right\|_{2}\right]
,

\tilde{P}_{i}^{k}:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})-M_{i,j}^{k}\right\|_{2}\right]
and

\tilde{S}_{i}^{k}:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{k}-G_{i,j}^{k}\right\|_{2}\right]
.
Then, Lemmas
7
,
8
, and the descent inequality above yield

\displaystyle\tilde{S}_{i}^{k+1}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{lemma:mg_rec_m_l0l1_no_p}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\tilde{S}_{i}^{k}+\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}\tilde{P}_{i}^{k}+\frac{t_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}L^{1}_{i,j}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]\right)

(23)

\displaystyle+\frac{t_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}+\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}\sigma_{i},

\displaystyle P_{i}^{k}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{lemma:nm_rec_m_l0l12}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1-\beta_{i})^{k}P_{i}^{0}+\frac{t_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}L^{1}_{i,j}\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}(1-\beta_{i})^{k-l}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{l})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

(24)

\displaystyle+\frac{t_{i}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}\beta_{i}}+\sigma_{i}\sqrt{\frac{\beta_{i}}{n}},

\displaystyle\tilde{P}_{i}^{k+1}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{lemma:nm_rec_m_l0l12}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1-\beta_{i})\tilde{P}_{i}^{k}+\frac{t_{i}(1-\beta_{i})}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}L^{1}_{i,j}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

(25)

\displaystyle+\frac{t_{i}(1-\beta_{i})\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}+\beta_{i}\sigma_{i},

\displaystyle\delta^{k+1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{k}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

(26)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}P_{i}^{k}+2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}\tilde{S}_{i}^{k}+\frac{t_{i}^{2}L^{0}_{i}}{2}+\frac{t_{i}^{2}L^{1}_{i}}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]\right).

Let
A_{i},B_{i}>0
be some constants to be determined later, and define

\displaystyle\Psi^{k}:=\delta^{k}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}\tilde{S}_{i}^{k}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}B_{i}\tilde{P}_{i}^{k}.

Then, using (
23
), (
25
) and (
26
)

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91692pt\Psi^{k+1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta^{k+1}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}\tilde{S}_{i}^{k+1}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}B_{i}\tilde{P}_{i}^{k+1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{k}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}P_{i}^{k}+2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}\tilde{S}_{i}^{k}+\frac{t_{i}^{2}L^{0}_{i}}{2}+\frac{t_{i}^{2}L^{1}_{i}}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}\left(\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\tilde{S}_{i}^{k}+\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}\tilde{P}_{i}^{k}+\frac{t_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}L^{1}_{i,j}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}\left(\frac{t_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}+\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}\sigma_{i}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}B_{i}\left((1-\beta_{i})\tilde{P}_{i}^{k}+\frac{t_{i}(1-\beta_{i})}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}L^{1}_{i,j}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]\right)+\frac{t_{i}(1-\beta_{i})\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}+\beta_{i}\sigma_{i}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta^{k}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}+A_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\right)\tilde{S}_{i}^{k}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(A_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}+B_{i}(1-\beta_{i})\right)\tilde{P}_{i}^{k}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}P_{i}^{k}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}^{2}L^{1}_{i}}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\left(A_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}+B_{i}(1-\beta_{i})\right)\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}L^{1}_{i,j}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}^{2}L^{0}_{i}}{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}\frac{t_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}B_{i}\frac{t_{i}(1-\beta_{i})\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}\sigma_{i}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}B_{i}\beta_{i}\sigma_{i}.

Taking
A_{i}=\frac{2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}
and
B_{i}=A_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}=\frac{2t_{i}\bar{\rho}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}
, we obtain

\displaystyle 2t_{i}\bar{\rho}+A_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}=2t_{i}\bar{\rho}+\frac{2t_{i}\bar{\rho}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}

\displaystyle=A_{i},

\displaystyle A_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}+B_{i}(1-\beta_{i})=A_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}+A_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}(1-\beta_{i})

\displaystyle=B_{i}.

Consequently,

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91692pt\Psi^{k+1}

(27)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{k}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}\tilde{S}_{i}^{k}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}B_{i}\tilde{P}_{i}^{k}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}P_{i}^{k}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}^{2}L^{1}_{i}}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{2t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}L^{1}_{i,j}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}^{2}L^{0}_{i}}{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{2t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{4t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}\sigma_{i}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:tudtuvhk}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\delta^{k}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{p}A_{i}\tilde{S}_{i}^{k}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}B_{i}\tilde{P}_{i}^{k}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}\left((1-\beta_{i})^{k}P_{i}^{0}+\frac{t_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}L^{1}_{i,j}\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}(1-\beta_{i})^{k-l}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{l})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]+\frac{t_{i}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}\beta_{i}}+\sigma_{i}\sqrt{\frac{\beta_{i}}{n}}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}^{2}L^{1}_{i}}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{2t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}L^{1}_{i,j}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}^{2}L^{0}_{i}}{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{2t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{4t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}\sigma_{i}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Psi^{k}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{p}2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}(1-\beta_{i})^{k}P_{i}^{0}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}^{2}L^{1}_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle+2\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}(1-\beta_{i})^{k-l}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}L^{1}_{i,j}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{l})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}L^{1}_{i,j}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]\right)+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}^{2}L^{0}_{i}}{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{2t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}\beta_{i}}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{2t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{4t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}\sigma_{i}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sigma_{i}\sqrt{\frac{\beta_{i}}{n}}.

Let us bound the terms involving the norms of the gradients. Using
Lemma

10
, we get

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}^{2}L^{1}_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{lemma:layer_gen_smooth3}}{\leq}

\displaystyle 4\max_{i\in[p]}(t_{i}^{2}(L^{1}_{i})^{2}){\mathbb{E}}\left[f(X^{k})-f^{\star}\right]+\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{p}(t_{i}^{2}L^{1}_{i})^{2}L_{i}^{0}}{\max_{i\in[p]}(t_{i}^{2}(L^{1}_{i})^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\max_{i\in[p]}(t_{i}^{2}(L^{1}_{i})^{2})\delta^{k}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}^{2}L_{i}^{0}.

Similarly,
Lemma

11
gives

\displaystyle\hskip-22.76219pt\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}(1-\beta_{i})^{k-l}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}L^{1}_{i,j}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{l})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}(1-\beta_{i})^{k-l}L^{1}_{i,j}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{l})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{lemma:layer_gen_smooth2}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}\left(4\max_{i\in[p]}\left(\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}(1-\beta_{i})^{k-l}(L_{i,j}^{1})^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[f_{j}(X^{l})-f^{\star}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}\left(4\max_{i\in[p]}\left(\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}(1-\beta_{i})^{k-l}(L_{i,j}^{1})^{2}\right)\left(f^{\star}-f_{j}^{\star}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}\left(\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}(1-\beta_{i})^{k-l}L^{1}_{i,j}\right)^{2}L_{i,j}^{0}}{\max_{i\in[p]}\left(\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}(1-\beta_{i})^{k-l}(L_{i,j}^{1})^{2}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}4\max_{i\in[p],j\in[n]}\left(\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}(1-\beta_{i})^{k-l}(L_{i,j}^{1})^{2}\right)\delta^{l}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}\left(4\max_{i\in[p]}\left(\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}(1-\beta_{i})^{k-l}(L_{i,j}^{1})^{2}\right)\left(f^{\star}-f_{j}^{\star}\right)+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}(1-\beta_{i})^{k-l}L_{i,j}^{0}\right)

and

\displaystyle\hskip-22.76219pt\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}L^{1}_{i,j}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}L^{1}_{i,j}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{lemma:layer_gen_smooth2}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(4\max_{i\in[p]}\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}(L^{1}_{i,j})^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[f_{j}(X^{k})-f^{\star}\right]+4\max_{i\in[p]}\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}(L^{1}_{i,j})^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\left(f^{\star}-f_{j}^{\star}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}L^{1}_{i,j}\right)^{2}L_{i,j}^{0}}{\max_{i\in[p]}\left(\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}(L_{i,j}^{1})^{2}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\max_{i\in[p],j\in[n]}\left(\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}(L^{1}_{i,j})^{2}\right)\delta^{k}+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(4\max_{i\in[p]}\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}(L^{1}_{i,j})^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\left(f^{\star}-f_{j}^{\star}\right)+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}L_{i,j}^{0}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\right).

Substituting these bounds in (
27
), we obtain

\displaystyle\hskip-17.07164pt\Psi^{k+1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Psi^{k}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{p}2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}(1-\beta_{i})^{k}P_{i}^{0}+2\max_{i\in[p]}(t_{i}^{2}(L^{1}_{i})^{2})\delta^{k}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}^{2}L_{i}^{0}

\displaystyle+8\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}\max_{i\in[p],j\in[n]}\left(\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}(1-\beta_{i})^{k-l}(L_{i,j}^{1})^{2}\right)\delta^{l}

\displaystyle+2\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}\left(4\max_{i\in[p]}\left(\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}(1-\beta_{i})^{k-l}(L_{i,j}^{1})^{2}\right)\left(f^{\star}-f_{j}^{\star}\right)+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}(1-\beta_{i})^{k-l}L_{i,j}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{8\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}\max_{i\in[p],j\in[n]}\left(\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}(L^{1}_{i,j})^{2}\right)\delta^{k}

\displaystyle+\frac{2\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(4\max_{i\in[p]}\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}(L^{1}_{i,j})^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\left(f^{\star}-f_{j}^{\star}\right)+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}L_{i,j}^{0}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\right)+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}^{2}L^{0}_{i}}{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{2t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}\beta_{i}}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{2t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{4t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}\sigma_{i}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sigma_{i}\sqrt{\frac{\beta_{i}}{n}}.

Since
\delta^{k}\leq\Psi^{k}
, it follows that

\displaystyle\hskip-17.07164pt\Psi^{k+1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1+2\max_{i\in[p]}(t_{i}^{2}(L^{1}_{i})^{2})+\frac{8\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}\max_{i\in[p],j\in[n]}\left(\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}(L^{1}_{i,j})^{2}\right)\right)\Psi^{k}

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{p}2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}(1-\beta_{i})^{k}P_{i}^{0}

\displaystyle+8\max_{i\in[p],j\in[n]}\left(\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}(L_{i,j}^{1})^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\right)\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}\max_{i\in[p]}((1-\beta_{i})^{k-l}\Psi^{l})

\displaystyle+8\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\max_{i\in[p]}\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}(L_{i,j}^{1})^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}\max_{i\in[p]}\left((1-\beta_{i})^{k-l}\right)\left(f^{\star}-f_{j}^{\star}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+2\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}\bar{L}_{i}^{0}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}(1-\beta_{i})^{k-l}+\frac{8\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\max_{i\in[p]}\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}(L^{1}_{i,j})^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\left(f^{\star}-f_{j}^{\star}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}\bar{L}_{i}^{0}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}^{2}L_{i}^{0}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{2t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}\beta_{i}}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{2t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{4t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}\sigma_{i}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sigma_{i}\sqrt{\frac{\beta_{i}}{n}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1+\underbrace{2\max_{i\in[p]}(t_{i}^{2}(L^{1}_{i})^{2})+\frac{8\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}\max_{i\in[p],j\in[n]}\left(\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}(L^{1}_{i,j})^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\right)}_{:=C_{1}}\right)\Psi^{k}

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{p}2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}(1-\beta_{i})^{k}P_{i}^{0}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{8\max_{i\in[p],j\in[n]}\left(\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}(L_{i,j}^{1})^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\right)}_{:=C_{2}}\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}((1-\beta_{\min})^{k-l}\Psi^{l})

\displaystyle+\underbrace{8\left(\frac{1}{\beta_{\min}}+\frac{\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\max_{i\in[p]}\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}(L^{1}_{i,j})^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\left(f^{\star}-f_{j}^{\star}\right)\right)}_{:=C_{3}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}^{2}\underbrace{\left(L_{i}^{0}+\frac{4\bar{\rho}_{i}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}\beta_{i}}+\frac{4\bar{\rho}_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})}\right)}_{:=C_{4,i}}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}\underbrace{\bar{\rho}_{i}\sigma_{i}\left(\frac{4\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}+2\sqrt{\frac{\beta_{i}}{n}}\right)}_{:=C_{5,i}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(1+C_{1}\right)\Psi^{k}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{p}2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}(1-\beta_{i})^{k}P_{i}^{0}

\displaystyle+C_{2}\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}((1-\beta_{\min})^{k-l}\Psi^{l})+C_{3}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}^{2}C_{4,i}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}C_{5,i}.

Now, define a weighting sequence
w^{k}:=\frac{w^{k-1}}{1+C_{1}+\frac{C_{2}}{\beta_{\min}}}
, where
w^{-1}=1
. Then, multiplying the above inequality by
w^{k}
and summing over the first
K+1
iterations, we obtain

\displaystyle\hskip-17.07164pt\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}\Psi^{k+1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}\left(1+C_{1}\right)\Psi^{k}+\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}C_{2}\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}((1-\beta_{\min})^{k-l}\Psi^{l})-\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}\sum_{i=1}^{p}2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}(1-\beta_{i})^{k}P_{i}^{0}+\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}C_{3}+\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}^{2}C_{4,i}+\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}C_{5,i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(1+C_{1}\right)\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}\Psi^{k}+C_{2}\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}((1-\beta_{\min})^{k-l}\Psi^{l})-\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}\sum_{i=1}^{p}2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}(1-\beta_{i})^{k}P_{i}^{0}+W^{K}C_{3}+W^{K}\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}^{2}C_{4,i}+W^{K}\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}C_{5,i}.

where
W^{K}:=\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}
. Since, by definition,
w^{k}\leq w^{k-1}\leq w^{-1}=1
, we have

\displaystyle\hskip-17.07164pt\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}\Psi^{k+1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1+C_{1}\right)\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}\Psi^{k}+C_{2}\sum_{k=0}^{K}\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}(w^{l}(1-\beta_{\min})^{k-l}\Psi^{l})-\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{K}\sum_{i=1}^{p}2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}(1-\beta_{i})^{k}P_{i}^{0}+W^{K}C_{3}+W^{K}\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}^{2}C_{4,i}+W^{K}\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}C_{5,i}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1+C_{1}\right)\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}\Psi^{k}+C_{2}\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}(1-\beta_{\min})^{l}\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}\Psi^{k}-\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle+2\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\beta_{i}}P_{i}^{0}+W^{K}C_{3}+W^{K}\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}^{2}C_{4,i}+W^{K}\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}C_{5,i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(1+C_{1}+\frac{C_{2}}{\beta_{\min}}\right)\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}\Psi^{k}-\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle+2\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\beta_{i}}P_{i}^{0}+W^{K}C_{3}+W^{K}\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}^{2}C_{4,i}+W^{K}\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}C_{5,i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k-1}\Psi^{k}-\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]+2\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\beta_{i}}P_{i}^{0}

\displaystyle+W^{K}C_{3}+W^{K}\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}^{2}C_{4,i}+W^{K}\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}C_{5,i}.

Rearranging the terms and dividing by
W^{K}
gives

\displaystyle\hskip-22.76219pt\min_{k=0,\ldots,K}\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{K}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{w^{k}}{W^{K}}t_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{W^{K}}\sum_{k=0}^{K}\left(w^{k-1}\Psi^{k}-w^{k}\Psi^{k+1}\right)+\frac{2}{W^{K}}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\beta_{i}}P_{i}^{0}+C_{3}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}^{2}C_{4,i}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}C_{5,i}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\Psi^{0}}{W^{K}}+\frac{2}{W^{K}}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\beta_{i}}P_{i}^{0}+C_{3}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}^{2}C_{4,i}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}C_{5,i}.

Now, note that

\displaystyle W^{K}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}\geq(K+1)w^{K}=\frac{(K+1)w^{-1}}{(1+C_{1}+\frac{C_{2}}{\beta_{\min}})^{K+1}}\geq\frac{K+1}{\exp\left((K+1)(C_{1}+\frac{C_{2}}{\beta_{\min}})\right)}.

Taking
t_{i}=\frac{\eta_{i}}{(K+1)^{3/4}}
, where
\eta_{i}^{2}\leq\min\left\{\frac{(K+1)^{1/2}}{6(L^{1}_{i})^{2}},\frac{(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})\underline{\rho}_{i}(K+1)^{1/2}}{24(K+1)\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\bar{\rho}_{i}(L^{1}_{i,\max})^{2}},\frac{\beta_{\min}\underline{\rho}_{i}(K+1)^{1/2}}{24\bar{\rho}_{i}(L_{i,\max}^{1})^{2}},1\right\}
to ensure that

\displaystyle 2(K+1)\max_{i\in[p]}(t_{i}^{2}(L^{1}_{i})^{2})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{3},

\displaystyle(K+1)\frac{8\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}\max_{i\in[p],j\in[n]}\left(\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}(L^{1}_{i,j})^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{3},

\displaystyle(K+1)\frac{8}{\beta_{\min}}\max_{i\in[p],j\in[n]}\left(\frac{t_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}(L^{1}_{i,j})^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{3},

we have
(K+1)(C_{1}+\frac{C_{2}}{\beta_{\min}})\leq 1
, and so
W^{K}\geq\frac{K+1}{\exp(1)}\geq\frac{K+1}{3}
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\hskip-22.76219pt\min_{k=0,\ldots,K}\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{3\Psi^{0}}{K+1}+\frac{6}{K+1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\beta_{i}}P_{i}^{0}+C_{3}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}^{2}C_{4,i}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}t_{i}C_{5,i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{3\Psi^{0}}{K+1}+\frac{6}{K+1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\beta_{i}(K+1)^{3/4}}P_{i}^{0}

\displaystyle+\frac{8}{(K+1)^{3/2}}\left(\frac{1}{\beta_{\min}}+\frac{\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\max_{i\in[p]}\frac{\eta_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}(L_{i,j}^{1})^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}\left(f^{\star}-f_{j}^{\star}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}^{2}}{(K+1)^{3/2}}\left(L_{i}^{0}+\frac{4\bar{\rho}_{i}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}\beta_{i}}+\frac{4\bar{\rho}_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}}{(K+1)^{3/4}}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sigma_{i}\left(\frac{4\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}+2\sqrt{\frac{\beta_{i}}{n}}\right).

Lastly, dividing by
\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}t_{l}=\frac{1}{(K+1)^{3/4}}\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}
gives

\displaystyle\hskip-22.76219pt\min_{k=0,\ldots,K}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{3\Psi^{0}}{(K+1)^{1/4}\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}+\frac{6}{K+1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}\frac{\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\beta_{i}}P_{i}^{0}

\displaystyle+\frac{8}{(K+1)^{3/4}}\left(\frac{1}{\beta_{\min}}+\frac{\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\frac{\max_{i\in[p]}\frac{\eta_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}(L_{i,j}^{1})^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}\left(f^{\star}-f_{j}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}^{2}}{(K+1)^{3/4}\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}\left(L_{i}^{0}+\frac{4\bar{\rho}_{i}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}\beta_{i}}+\frac{4\bar{\rho}_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sigma_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}\left(\frac{4\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\beta_{i}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}+2\sqrt{\frac{\beta_{i}}{n}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{3\Psi^{0}}{(K+1)^{1/4}\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}+\frac{6}{(K+1)^{1/2}}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}P_{i}^{0}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{8}{(K+1)^{1/4}}+\frac{8\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})(K+1)^{3/4}}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\frac{\max_{i\in[p]}\frac{\eta_{i}^{2}\bar{\rho}_{i}(L_{i,j}^{1})^{2}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}\left(f^{\star}-f_{j}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}^{2}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}\left(\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{(K+1)^{3/4}}+\frac{4\bar{\rho}_{i}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}(K+1)^{1/4}}+\frac{4\bar{\rho}_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})(K+1)^{3/4}}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sigma_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}\left(\frac{4\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})(K+1)^{1/2}}+\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}(K+1)^{1/4}}\right),

where in the last equality we set
\beta_{i}=\frac{1}{(K+1)^{1/2}}
.
∎

Proof of
Corollary

3
.

Substituting the initialization, we have

\displaystyle P_{i}^{0}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{0})-M_{i}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right]={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0};\xi_{j}^{0})\right)\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0};\xi_{j}^{0})\right)\right\|^{2}_{2}\right]}\overset{\eqref{as:bounded_layer_var}}{\leq}\frac{\sigma_{i}}{\sqrt{n}},

\displaystyle\tilde{P}_{i}^{0}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0})-M_{i,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right]=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0};\xi_{j}^{0})\right\|_{2}\right]\leq\sigma_{i},

\displaystyle\tilde{S}_{i}^{0}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{0}-G_{i,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right]=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0};\xi_{j}^{0})-\mathcal{C}_{i,j}^{0}(\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0};\xi_{j}^{0}))\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{def:contractive_compressor}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0};\xi_{j}^{0})\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0};\xi_{j}^{0})-\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0})\right\|_{2}\right]\overset{\eqref{as:bounded_layer_var}}{\leq}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\sigma_{i},

and hence

\displaystyle\Psi^{0}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle f(X^{0})-f^{\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|M_{i,j}^{0}-G_{i,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f_{j}(X^{0})-M_{i,j}^{0}\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle f(X^{0})-f^{\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\sigma_{i}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{2t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}\sigma_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f(X^{0})-f^{\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{4\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}t_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sigma_{i}}{1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}.

Substituting this in the rate, we get

\displaystyle\hskip-22.76219pt\min_{k=0,\ldots,K}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X^{k})\right\|_{(i)\star}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{3}{(K+1)^{1/4}\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}\left(f(X^{0})-f^{\star}+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{4\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\eta_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sigma_{i}}{(K+1)^{3/4}(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{6}{(K+1)^{1/2}}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\bar{\rho}_{i}\eta_{i}\sigma_{i}}{\sqrt{n}\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{8}{(K+1)^{1/4}}+\frac{8\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{(K+1)^{3/4}(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\frac{\max_{i\in[p]}\eta_{i}^{2}\frac{\bar{\rho}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}}(L_{i,j}^{1})^{2}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}\left(f^{\star}-f_{j}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}^{2}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}\left(\frac{L_{i}^{0}}{(K+1)^{3/4}}+\frac{4\bar{\rho}_{i}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}(K+1)^{1/4}}+\frac{4\bar{\rho}_{i}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}\bar{L}^{0}_{i}}{\underline{\rho}_{i}(K+1)^{3/4}(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\eta_{i}\bar{\rho}_{i}\sigma_{i}}{\frac{1}{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\eta_{l}}\left(\frac{4\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}}}{(K+1)^{1/2}(1-\sqrt{1-\alpha_{D}})}+\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}(K+1)^{1/4}}\right).

∎

Appendix F
Useful Facts and Lemmas

For all
X,Y\in\mathcal{S}
,
t>0
and
\alpha\in(0,1]
, we have:

\displaystyle\left\|X+Y\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1+t)\left\|X\right\|^{2}+(1+t^{-1})\left\|Y\right\|^{2},

(28)

\displaystyle\left\langle X,Y\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left\|X\right\|^{2}}{2t}+\frac{t\left\|Y\right\|^{2}}{2},

(29)

\displaystyle\left(1-\alpha\right)\left(1+\frac{\alpha}{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 1-\frac{\alpha}{2},

(30)

\displaystyle\left(1-\alpha\right)\left(1+\frac{2}{\alpha}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\alpha},

(31)

\displaystyle\left\langle G,{\rm LMO}_{\mathcal{B}(X,t)}\left(G\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle=-t\left\|G\right\|_{\star}

(32)

\displaystyle\left\langle X,X^{\sharp}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\|X^{\sharp}\right\|^{2},

(33)

\displaystyle\left\|X\right\|_{\star}

\displaystyle=\left\|X^{\sharp}\right\|.

(34)

Lemma 12

(
Riabinin et al. 2025b
, Lemma 3)
.

Suppose that
x_{1},\ldots,x_{p},y_{1},\ldots,y_{p}\in\mathbb{R}
,
\max_{i\in[p]}|x_{i}|>0
and
z_{1},\ldots,z_{p}>0
. Then

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{y_{i}^{2}}{z_{i}}\geq\frac{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}x_{i}y_{i}\right)^{2}}{\sum_{i=1}^{p}z_{i}x_{i}^{2}}.

Lemma 13

(Variance decomposition)
.

For any random vector
X\in\mathcal{S}
and any non-random
c\in\mathcal{S}
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X-c\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|X-{\mathbb{E}}\left[X\right]\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]+\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[X\right]-c\right\|_{2}^{2}.

Lemma 14

(
Riabinin et al. 2025b
, Lemma 1)
.

Let
8
hold. Then, for any
X,Y\in\mathcal{S}
,

\displaystyle\left|f(Y)-f(X)-\left\langle\nabla f(X),Y-X\right\rangle\right|\leq\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{L^{0}_{i}+L^{1}_{i}\left\|\nabla_{i}f(X)\right\|_{(i)\star}}{2}\left\|X_{i}-Y_{i}\right\|_{(i)}^{2}.

Lemma 15
.

Let
\{A^{k}\}_{k\geq 0}
,
\{B_{i}^{k}\}_{k\geq 0}
,
i\in[p]
be non-negative sequences such that

\displaystyle A^{k+1}\leq\left(1+a_{1}\right)A^{k}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}B_{i}^{k}+a_{2},

where
a_{1},a_{2}\geq 0
. Then

\displaystyle\min_{k=0,\ldots,K}\sum_{i=1}^{p}B_{i}^{k}\leq\frac{\exp(a_{1}(K+1))}{(K+1)}A^{0}+a_{2}.

Proof.

Let us define a weighting sequence
w^{k}:=\frac{w^{k-1}}{1+a_{1}}
, where
w^{-1}=1
. Then

\displaystyle w^{k}A^{k+1}\leq w^{k}\left(1+a_{1}\right)A^{k}-w^{k}\sum_{i=1}^{p}B_{i}^{k}+w^{k}a_{2}=w^{k-1}A^{k}-w^{k}\sum_{i=1}^{p}B_{i}^{k}+w^{k}a_{2},

and hence

\displaystyle\min_{k=0,\ldots,K}\sum_{i=1}^{p}B_{i}^{k}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}}\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}\sum_{i=1}^{p}B_{i}^{k}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}}\sum_{k=0}^{K}\left(w^{k-1}A^{k}-w^{k}A^{k+1}\right)+\frac{1}{\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}}\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}a_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}}\left(w^{-1}A^{0}-w^{K}A^{K+1}\right)+a_{2}.

Using the fact that
w^{-1}=1
and
\sum_{k=0}^{K}w^{k}=\sum_{k=0}^{K}\frac{1}{(1+a_{1})^{k+1}}\geq\frac{K+1}{(1+a_{1})^{K+1}}
, we get

\displaystyle\min_{k=0,\ldots,K}\sum_{i=1}^{p}B_{i}^{k}\leq\frac{(1+a_{1})^{K+1}}{(K+1)}\left(A^{0}-w^{K}A^{K+1}\right)+a_{2}\leq\frac{\exp(a_{1}(K+1))}{(K+1)}A^{0}+a_{2},

which finishes the proof.
∎

G.2
Top
K
Compression Details

Top
K
compressor requires transmitting both the selected values and their corresponding indices to reconstruct the original tensors. At high compression levels, this introduces significant communication overhead, especially in compositional schemes such as Top
K
combined with the Natural compressor, where the cost of transmitting indices can even exceed that of the quantized values. To illustrate this effect, we analyze the largest parameter matrices in the
NanoGPT
model: the token embedding layer and the classification head, each of size
50,304\times 768
. Representing an index for any element in these matrices requires
\log_{2}(50{,}304\cdot 768)<26
bits. We use this calculation when visualizing communication costs.

G.3
Learning Rate Ablation

To ensure a fair and robust comparison, we perform a learning rate hyperparameter sweep for each compression configuration, as detailed in
Figure

3
. For every method, the search space is initialized at the optimal learning rate of the uncompressed baseline (taken from the
Gluon
repository
[
Riabinin et al. 2025a
]
) and spans downward by up to an order of magnitude. We consistently observe that more aggressive compression schemes require a smaller learning rate for stable convergence.

This tuning protocol is applied uniformly across all experiments for models trained with 2.5B (
Section

G.5
) and 5B token budgets.

Figure 3:
Learning rate ablation.
The grid spans from the optimal learning rate of the non-compressed baseline,
3.6\times 10^{-4}
(denoted as
1.0\times
), down to
0.1\times
. Red curves correspond to experiments processing 5B tokens (
Section

5
), while blue curves correspond to 2.5B tokens (
Section

G.5
).

G.6
Limitations

Reporting results for all compressors on the same token budget (for instance, 5B) and then measuring the prefix needed to reach a given loss threshold may not be fully consistent, as results can be affected by the scheduler. To mitigate this, we use a relatively strong loss threshold that ensures a significant number of tokens are processed beyond the constant learning rate phase. Additionally, tuning the initial learning rate can help stabilize the results.
